Ross’s head came up. He was listening, not to anything in that cabin, but to a sound which had come through the port just behind his head. There—it had come again! He was on his feet.

===Chinese===
“没有。造出这样的容器、这样的听音器，费了我族人许多年和许多人的学识合力。我没有这个。”

“那何必去想我们没有的东西？”托尔古尔的回应斩钉截铁。“我们*有*什么？”

罗斯霍然抬头。他在听的，不是舱里的任何动静，而是从他脑后舷窗外传来的一种声响。听——又来了！他一跃而起。

===Original===
“What—?” Vistur’s hand hovered over the ax at his belt. Ross saw their gaze centered on him.

“We may have reinforcements now!” The Terran was already on his way to the deck.

He hurried to the rail and whistled, the thin, shrill summons he had practiced for weeks before he had ever begun this fantastic adventure.

A sleek dark body broke water and the dolphin grin was exposed as Tino-rau answered his call. Though Ross’s communication powers with the two finned scouts was very far from Karara’s, he caught the message in part and swung around to face the Rovers who had crowded after him.

===Chinese===
“怎么——？”维斯图尔的手悬在腰间的斧柄上。罗斯发觉众人的目光都聚在自己身上。

“我们可能有援军了！”特兰人已经直奔甲板而去。

他奔到栏杆边吹起哨音——那细而尖的召唤，早在踏上这场离奇冒险之前，他就练了好几个星期。

一个流线的黑影破水而出，蒂诺-劳应声而现，露出海豚的笑。虽然罗斯与两位带鳍斥候沟通的本事远不及卡拉拉，他还是听懂了部分讯息，转身面对跟出来围住的掠海者。

===Original===
“We have a way now of learning more about your enemies.”

“A boat—it comes without sail or oars!” One of the crew pointed.

Ross waved vigorously, but no hand replied from the skiff. Though it came steadily onward, the three cruisers its apparent goal.

“Karara!” Ross called.

===Chinese===
“现在我们有了法子，可以进一步摸清你们敌人的底细。”

“一条船——没帆没桨地过来了！”一名船员指着说。

罗斯使劲挥手，小艇上却没有手回应。它虽一路稳稳驶来，目标显然是这三艘巡洋舰。

“卡拉拉！”罗斯喊道。

===Original===
Then side by side with Tino-rau were two wet heads, two masked faces showing as the swimmers trod water—Karara and Loketh.

“Drop ropes!” Ross gave that order as if he rather than Torgul commanded. And the Captain himself was one of those who moved to obey.

Loketh came out of the sea first and as he scrambled over the rail he had his sword ready, looking from Ross to Torgul. The Terran held up empty hands and smiled.

===Chinese===
随即，蒂诺-劳旁边冒出两个湿漉漉的脑袋，两张戴面具的脸在踩水的泳者之间显露出来——卡拉拉和洛凯斯。

“抛绳！”罗斯下达这道命令，俨然发号施令的是他而不是托尔古尔。而船长本人也正是上前听命的人之一。

洛凯斯先出了水，翻过栏杆时剑已握在手里，目光在罗斯和托尔古尔之间来回。特兰人举起空空的双手，笑了。

===Original===
“No trouble now.”

Loketh snapped up his mask. “So the Sea Maid said the finned ones reported. Yet before, these thirsted for your blood on their blades. What magic have you worked?”

“None. Just the truth has been discovered.” Ross reached for Karara’s hand as she came nimbly up the rope, swung her across the rail to the deck where she stood unmasked, brushing back her hair and looking around with a lively curiosity.

===Chinese===
“现在没麻烦了。”

洛凯斯把面罩往上一掀。“海之少女也这么说，带鳍的报的信。可先前，这些人还巴不得刀刃上饮你的血。你使了什么魔法？”

“没使魔法。只是真相大白了。”卡拉拉正敏捷地攀绳而上，罗斯伸手拉她，把她荡过栏杆送上甲板。她站在那里揭了面罩，拢了拢头发，带着鲜活的好奇环顾四周。

===Original===
“Karara, this is Captain Torgul,” Ross introduced the Rover commander who was staring round-eyed at the girl. “Karara is she who swims with the finned ones, and they obey her.” Ross gestured to Tino-rau. “It is Taua who brings the skiff?” he asked the Polynesian.

She nodded. “We followed from the gate. Then Loketh came and said that … that.…” She paused and then added, “But you do not seem to be in danger. What has happened?”

“Much. Listen—this is important. There is trouble at an island ahead. The Baldies were there; they murdered the kin of these men. The odds are they reached there by some form of sub. Send one of the dolphins to see what is happening and if they are still there. …”

===Chinese===
“卡拉拉，这位是托尔古尔船长。”罗斯介绍那位圆睁双眼盯着姑娘的掠海者统帅。“卡拉拉就是与带鳍者同游的人，它们听她的号令。”罗斯朝蒂诺-劳比了比。“是小艇由塔乌亚送来的吗？”他问那位波利尼西亚姑娘。

她点头。“我们从那道门一路跟来。后来洛凯斯来了，说……说那些。”她顿了顿，又补上一句，“可你看上去并没有身处险境。出什么事了？”

“出了不少事。听着——这很重要。前方一座岛上出了乱子。秃头人去过那里；他们杀害了这些人的亲族。十有八九，他们是坐某种潜艇到的。派一只海豚去看看动静，看看他们还在不在……”

===Original===
Karara asked no more questions, but whistled to the dolphin. With a flip of tail Tino-rau took off.

Since they could make no concrete plan of action, the cruiser captains agreed to wait for Tino-rau’s report and to cruise well out of sight of the fairing harbor until it came.

“This belief in magic,” Ross remarked to Karara, “has one advantage. The natives seem able to take in their stride the fact the dolphins will scout for us.”

===Chinese===
卡拉拉不再多问，朝海豚吹了声哨。蒂诺-劳尾鳍一摆，绝尘而去。

既然拿不出具体的行动计划，几位巡洋舰船长商定等蒂诺-劳的报告，在此之前远远兜圈子，保持在站港港口的视界之外。

“这种对魔法的信仰，”罗斯对卡拉拉说，“倒有一样好处。海豚要替我们侦察，这档子事土著们似乎能处之泰然。”

===Original===
“They have lived their lives on the sea; for it they must have a vast respect. Perhaps they know, as did my people, that the ocean has many secrets, some of which are never revealed except to the forms of life which claim their homes there. But, even if you discover this Baldy sub, what will the Rovers be able to do about it?”

“I don’t know—yet.” Ross could not tell why he clung to the idea that they could do anything to strike back at the superior alien force. He only knew that he was not yet willing to relinquish the thought that in some way they could.

===Chinese===
“他们一辈子泡在海上；对海洋必怀深深的敬畏。也许他们和我族人一样明白，大洋藏着许多秘密，其中一些，只向以海为家的那些生灵显露。不过，就算你找到这艘秃头人的潜艇，掠海者们又能拿它怎么样？”

“我不知道——现在还不知道。”罗斯说不清自己为什么死抱着一个念头：他们总能为回击那支占尽优势的外星力量做点什么。他只知道，自己还不肯放弃这个念头——总有某种办法。

===Original===
“And Ashe?”

Yes, Ashe. …

“I don’t know.” It hurt Ross to admit that.

“Back there, what really happened at the gate?” he asked Karara. “All at once the dolphins seemed to go crazy.”

===Chinese===
“那阿什呢？”

是啊，阿什……

“我不知道。”承认这一点，罗斯心里发疼。

“回到先前——那道门那儿到底出了什么事？”他问卡拉拉。“海豚们怎么突然像疯了一样。”

===Original===
“I think for a moment or two they did. You felt nothing?”

“No.”

“It was like a fire slashing through the head. Some protective device of the Foanna, I think.”

A mental defense to which he was not sensitive. Which meant that he might be able to breach that gate if none of the others could. But he had to be there first. Suppose, just suppose Torgul could be persuaded that this attack on the gutted Kyn Add was useless. Would the Rover commander take them back to the Foanna keep? Or with the dolphins and the skiff could Ross himself return to make the try?

===Chinese===
“我想有那么一两刻，它们确实疯了。你什么都没觉着？”

“没有。”

“那感觉像一把火横着劈过脑袋。我想，是福安娜的某种护门装置。”

一种他不受影响的心智防御。这意味着，如果旁人都过不去，他或许能攻破那道门。可他得先到那里才行。设想一下——仅仅是设想——倘若能说动托尔古尔，让他相信对已被血洗的基恩-阿德兴师动众毫无用处，这位掠海者统帅会不会送他们回福安娜的堡垒？或者，靠着海豚和小艇，罗斯自己能不能回去试上一试？

===Original===
That he could make it on his own, Ross doubted. Excitement and will power had buoyed him up throughout the past Hawaikan day and night. Now fatigue closed in, past his conditioning and the built-in stimulant of the Terran rations, to enclose him in a groggy haze. He had been warned against this reaction, but that was just another item he had pushed out of his conscious mind. The last thing he remembered now was seeing Karara move through a fuzzy cloud.

Voices argued somewhere beyond, the force of that argument carried more by tone than any words Ross could understand. He was pulled sluggishly out of a slumber too deep for any dream to trouble, and lifted heavy eyelids to see Karara once again. There was a prick in his arm—or was that part of the unreality about him?

“—four—five—six—” she was counting, and Ross found himself joining in:

===Chinese===
单枪匹马能不能成，罗斯心里没底。过去哈瓦伊卡的一昼一夜，亢奋和意志力一直托着他。此刻疲惫合围过来，越过了他的体能训练，也越过了特拉口粮里内置的提神剂，把他裹进一层晕沉的雾里。这种反应他早被告诫过，可那不过是又被他挤出意识的又一桩事。他记得的最后一幕，是卡拉拉的身影在毛茸茸的云雾里晃动。

什么地方有人在争吵，那争吵的分量多半在腔调里，而不在罗斯听得懂的任何字眼里。他被慢吞吞地拽出一场深得连梦都搅不动的酣睡，抬起沉重的眼皮，又看见了卡拉拉。手臂上有一记刺痛——还是说，那也只是周遭虚幻的一部分？

“——四——五——六——”她在数着数，罗斯发觉自己也跟着数了起来：

===Original===
“—seven—eight—nine—ten!”

On reaching “ten” he was fully awake and knew that she had applied the emergency procedure they had been drilled in using, giving him a pep shot. When Ross sat up on the narrow bunk there was a light in the cabin and no sign of day outside the porthole. Torgul, Vistur, the two other cruiser captains, all there … and Jazia.

Ross swung his feet to the deck. A pep-shot headache was already beginning, but would wear off soon. There was, however, a concentration of tension in the cabin, and something must have driven Karara to use the drug.

===Chinese===
“——七——八——九——十！”

数到“十”，他彻底醒了，明白她使的是他们演练过的紧急处置——给他打了一针提振剂。罗斯在狭窄的铺位上坐起身，舱里有灯，舷窗外不见天光。托尔古尔、维斯图尔、另外两位巡洋舰船长，全都在……还有贾齐娅。

罗斯把脚放到甲板上。提神针的头痛已经冒头，不过很快会散。然而舱里凝着一团紧张，必是出了什么事，才逼得卡拉拉用上了针剂。

===Original===
“What is it?”

Karara fitted the medical kit into the compact carrying case.

“Tino-rau has returned. There *is* a sub in the bay. It emits energy waves on a shoreward beam.”

“Then they are still there.” Ross accepted the dolphin’s report without question. Neither of the scouts would make a mistake in those matters. Energy waves beamed shoreward—power for some type of unit the Baldies were using? Suppose the Rovers could find a way of cutting off the power.

===Chinese===
“怎么了？”

卡拉拉把医疗包收进便携的匣子。

“蒂诺-劳回来了。湾里*确实*有一艘潜艇。它朝岸上定向发射能量波。”

“那他们还在。”罗斯对海豚的报告照单全收。两位斥候在这种事情上绝不会出错。能量波定向朝岸——是供给秃头人使用的某种装置的能源？假如掠海者能找到切断这能源的法子。

===Original===
“The Sea Maid has told us that this ship sits on the bottom of the harbor. If we could board it—” began Torgul.

“Yes!” Vistur brought his fist down against the end of the bunk on which the Terran still sat, jarring the dull, drug-borne pain in Ross’s head. “Take it—then turn it against its crew!”

There was an eagerness in all Rover faces. For that was a game the Hawaikan seafarers understood: Take an enemy ship and turn its armament against its companions in a fleet. But that plan would not work out. Ross had a healthy respect for the technical knowledge of the galactic invaders. Of course he, Karara, even Loketh might be able to reach the sub. Whether they could then board her was an entirely different matter.

===Chinese===
“海之少女告诉我们，那船就坐在港底。要是我们能登上去——”托尔古尔开了个头。

“对！”维斯图尔一拳砸在特兰人还坐着的那张铺位尾端，震得罗斯脑中那阵药力带来的钝痛直晃。“夺下它——再调转矛头打它的船员！”

所有掠海者的脸上都烧着热望。因为那是哈瓦伊卡海上人玩得转的把戏：夺下敌舰，把它的火力调头轰击舰队里的同伙。但这计划行不通。罗斯对银河入侵者的技术功底怀有清醒的敬意。他、卡拉拉、甚至洛凯斯，或许真能摸到潜艇边上。可登不登得上去，就完全是另一回事了。

===Original===
Now the Polynesian girl shook her head. “The broadcast there—Tino-rau rates it as lethal. There are dead fish floating in the bay. He had warning at the reef entrance. Without a shield, there will be no way of getting in.”

“Might as well wish for a depth bomb,” Ross began and then stopped.

“You have thought of something?”

===Chinese===
这时波利尼西亚姑娘摇头。“那边的辐射——蒂诺-劳判定是致命的。湾里漂着死鱼。它在礁口就收到了警告。没有护盾，根本进不去。”

“那还不如指望天上掉下一颗深水炸弹。”罗斯刚说到一半，停住了。

“你想到什么了？”

===Original===
“A shield—” Ross repeated her words. It was so wild this thought of his, and one which might have no chance of working. He knew almost nothing about the resources of the invaders. Could that broadcast which protected the sub and perhaps activated the weapons of the invaders ashore be destroyed? A wall of fish—sea life herded in there as a shield … wild, yes, even so wild it might work. Ross outlined the idea, speaking more to Karara than to the Rovers.

“I do not know,” she said doubtfully. “That would need many fish, too many to herd and drive—”

“Not fish,” Torgul cut in, “salkars!”

“Salkars?”

===Chinese===
“护盾——”罗斯重复她的话。他这个念头荒唐透顶，而且未必有一线成功的可能。他对入侵者的家底几乎一无所知。那道护住潜艇、说不定还为岸上的入侵者兵器供能的辐射，能被摧毁吗？一道鱼墙——把海中生灵驱赶到那里充当护盾……荒唐，是荒唐，可荒唐到没准真能成。罗斯把这个主意讲了一遍，话多半是说给卡拉拉，不是说给掠海者听的。

“我不知道，”她将信将疑。“那得要好多鱼，多得赶不过来、驱不动——”

“不要鱼，”托尔古尔插进来，“要萨尔卡！”

“萨尔卡？”

===Original===
“You have seen the bow carving on this ship. That is a salkar. Such are larger than a hundred fish! Salkars driven in … they might even wreck this undersea ship with their weight and anger.”

“And you can find these salkars nearby?” Ross began to take fire. That dragon which had hunted him—the bulk of the thing was well above any other sea life he had seen here. And to its ferocity he could give testimony.

“At the spawning reefs. We do not hunt at this season which is the time of the taking of mates. Now, too, they are easily angered so they will even attack a cruiser. To slay them at present is a loss, for their skins are not good. But they would be ripe for battle were they to be disturbed.”

===Chinese===
“你见过这条船船首的雕像。那就是萨尔卡。这种东西比一百条鱼加起来还大！把萨尔卡群驱进去……凭它们的分量和怒火，没准能把这艘水下船撞个稀烂。”

“附近找得到这些萨尔卡？”罗斯的心火也烧了起来。那头猎杀过他的龙——那家伙的块头，远超他在这里见过的任何海中生灵。它的凶悍，他可以出面作证。

“在产卵礁。这个季节我们不猎它们，眼下正是它们求偶的时候。这时候它们也最易被激怒，甚至会主动攻击巡洋舰。此时杀它们是亏本的，皮子不好。可只要惊扰一下，它们便正是一触即战的火药。”

===Original===
“And how would you get them from the spawning reefs to Kyn Add?”

“That is not too difficult; the reef lies here.” Torgul drew lines with the point of his sword on the table top. “And here is Kyn Add. Salkars have a great hunger at this time. Show them bait and they will follow; especially will they follow swimming bait.”

There were a great many holes in the plan which had only a halfway chance of working. But the Rovers seized upon it with enthusiasm, and so it was set up.

===Chinese===
“那你们怎么把它们从产卵礁弄到基恩-阿德去？”

“这不难；礁在这里。”托尔古尔用剑尖在桌面上划线。“这里是基恩-阿德。萨尔卡这时候胃口极大。给它们看饵，它们就会追；尤其是活的、游着的饵，追得更紧。”

这计划破绽百出，成功的把握顶多一半。但掠海者们满腔热忱地抓住了它，于是便这么定了下来。

===Original===
Perhaps some two hours later Ross swam toward the land mass of Kyn Add. Gleams of light pricked on the shore well to his left. Those must mark the Rover settlement. And again the Terran wondered why the invaders had remained there. Unless they knew that there had been three cruisers out on a raid and for some reason they were determined to make a complete mop-up.

Karara moved a little to his right, Taua between them, the dolphin’s super senses their guide and warning. The swiftest of the cruisers had departed, Loketh on board to communicate with Tino-rau in the water. Since the male dolphin was the best equipped to provide a fox for salkar hounds, he was the bait for this weird fishing expedition.

===Chinese===
约莫两个钟头后，罗斯朝基恩-阿德的陆块游去。左手远处，岸上闪烁着点点光亮。那想必是掠海者的聚落。特兰人又纳闷起来，入侵者为什么留在那里。除非他们知道有三艘巡洋舰在外袭扰，而且出于某种缘故，铁了心要斩草除根。

卡拉拉游在他右侧稍远处，塔乌亚居中，海豚的超常感官是他们的向导和警戒。最快的那艘巡洋舰已经出发，洛凯斯随船，负责与水中的蒂诺-劳联络。既然雄海豚最有本事给萨尔卡这群猎犬当那只狐狸，这场古怪渔猎的饵，便由它来当。

===Original===
“No farther!” Ross’s sonic pricked a warning against his body. Through that he took a jolt which sent him back, away from the bay entrance.

“On the reef.” Karara’s tapped code drew him on a new course. Moments later they were both out of the water, though the wash of waves over their flippered feet was constant. The rocks among which they crouched were a rough harborage from which they could see the shore as a dark blot. But they were well away from the break in the reef through which, if their outlandish plan succeeded, the salkars would come.

“A one-in-a-million chance!” Ross commented as he put up his mask.

===Chinese===
“不能再进了！”罗斯身上的声波器贴着皮肤刺出警示。透过那道警示他又挨了一记震荡，直把他推了回去，退出湾口。

“上礁石。”卡拉拉敲出的暗号把他引上新路线。片刻之后两人都出了水，脚蹼上浪花不断冲刷。他们蹲伏的乱石是一处粗陋的栖身地，从那里能望见岸上黑黢黢的一团。但他们离礁上那道裂口远远的——倘若这个异想天开的计划得逞，萨尔卡群便会从那里进来。

“百万分之一的胜算！”罗斯掀起面罩，评了一句。

===Original===
“Was not the whole Time Agent project founded on just such chances?” Karara asked the right question. This was Ross’s kind of venture. Yes, one-in-a-million chances had been pulled off by the Time Agents. Why, it had been close to those odds against their ever finding what they had first sought along the back trails of time—the wrecked spaceships.

Just suppose this could be a rehearsal for another attack? If the salkars could be made to crack the guard of the Baldies, could they also be used against the Foanna gate? Maybe. … But take one fight at a time.

“They come!” Karara’s fingers gripped Ross’s shoulder. Her hand was hard, bar rigid. He could see nothing, hear nothing. That warning must have come from the dolphins. But so far their plan was working; the monsters of the Hawaikan sea were on their way.

===Chinese===
“整个时间特工计划，难道不正是建立在这样的胜算之上？”卡拉拉问到了点子上。这正是罗斯那种买卖。是啊，时间特工们曾把一个个百万分之一变成了现实。可不是嘛，当初他们沿时间的来路去寻最初要找的东西——那些失事飞船——胜算之渺茫，与此几乎不相上下。

设想一下，这能不能当作另一场进攻的预演？如果萨尔卡群能被用来砸开秃头人的守卫，那它们能不能同样用来对付福安娜的门？也许……但一场仗一场仗地打。

“它们来了！”卡拉拉的手指扣住罗斯的肩膀。那只手硬得像一根铁棍。他什么也看不见，什么也听不见。那警报必定来自海豚。但到目前为止，他们的计划正在生效；哈瓦伊卡海洋的巨怪，已经上路了。

## XII Baldies / XII 秃头人

===Original===
“Ohhhh!” Karara clutched at Ross, her breath coming in little gasps, giving vent to her fear and horror. They had not known what might come from this plan; certainly neither had foreseen the present chaos in the lagoon.

Perhaps the broadcast energy of the enemy whipped the already vicious-tempered salkars into this insane fury. But now the moonlit water was beaten into foam as the creatures fought there, attacking each other with a ferocity neither Terran had witnessed before.

Lights gleamed along the shore where the alien invaders must have been drawn by the clamor of the fighting marine reptiles. Somewhere in the heights above the beach of the lagoon a picked band of Rovers should now be making their way from the opposite side of Kyn Add under strict orders not to go into attack unless signaled. Whether the independent sea warriors would hold to that command was a question which had worried Ross from the first.

Tino-rau and Taua in the waters to the seaward of the reef, the two Terrans on that barrier itself, and between them and the shore the wild melee of maddened salkars. Ross started. The sonic warning which had been pulsing steadily against his skin cut off sharply. The broadcast in the bay had been silenced! This was the time to move, but no swimmer could last in the lagoon itself.

===Chinese===
“噢——！”卡拉拉一把抓住罗斯，呼吸一抽一抽地急促，宣泄着她的恐惧与惊骇。他们原本并不知道这计划会引出什么，眼下潟湖里这场大乱，两人谁都不曾料到。

也许是敌人的辐射能量，把本就凶性天成的萨尔卡激成了这般疯狂怒焰。此刻月光下的水面被打成一片白沫，那些家伙在其中自相厮杀，那份凶狠暴烈，两个特兰人都不曾亲眼见过。

沿岸灯火闪烁，异星入侵者想必是被海中爬虫厮杀的喧嚣引了出来。潟湖滩上方的高地里，此刻应有一队精选的掠海者正从基恩-阿德的另一侧摸近，奉了严令：不见讯号，不得出击。这些桀骜不驯的海上武士到底会不会恪守军令，是从一开始就让罗斯悬心的问题。

蒂诺-劳和塔乌亚在礁外海水中，两个特兰人在礁石屏障上，他们与海岸之间，便是发狂萨尔卡的混战乱局。罗斯一怔。贴着他皮肤平稳搏动的声波警示骤然中断。湾里的辐射被掐灭了！正是行动的时机，可任凭哪路游泳好手，也无法在潟湖本体里撑下去。

===Original===
“Along the reef,” Karara said.

That would be the long way round, Ross knew, but the only one possible. He studied the cluster of lights ashore. Two or three figures moved there. Seemingly the attention of the aliens was well centered upon the battle still in progress in the lagoon.

“Stay here!” he ordered the girl. Adjusting his mask, Ross dropped into the water, cutting away from the reef and then turning to swim parallel with it. Tino-rau matched him as he went, guiding Ross to a second break in the reef, toward the shore some distance from where the conflict of the salkars still made a hideous din in the night.

The Terran waded in the shallows, stripping off his flippers and snapping them to his belt, letting his mask swing free on his chest. He angled toward the beach where the aliens had been. At least he was better armed for this than he had been when he had fronted the Rovers with only a diver’s knife. From the Time Agent supplies he had taken the single hand weapon he had long ago found in the armory of the derelict spaceship. This could only be used sparingly, since they did not know how it could be recharged, and the secret of its beam still remained secret as far as Terran technicians were concerned.

===Chinese===
“沿着礁石走。”卡拉拉说。

罗斯知道这要绕远路，却是唯一走得通的一条。他细看岸上那簇灯火。有两三个人影在移动。异星人的注意力似乎全拴在潟湖里仍在进行的战斗上。

“你待在这儿！”他命令姑娘。罗斯整好面罩，翻身入水，先斜刺里游离礁石，再转身与它平行游进。蒂诺-劳与他并驾齐游，一路把罗斯引向礁石的第二道裂口，朝岸上而去——那地方离萨尔卡厮杀声仍在夜里响成一片骇人喧嚣的所在，还隔着一段路。

特兰人在浅水里蹚行，剥下脚蹼别上腰带，任面罩荡在胸前。他斜斜朝异星人先前所在的那片海滩进发。至少这一趟，他的武装比当初只凭一把潜水刀面对掠海者时强得多了。他从时间特工的补给里取来了那件手兵器——多年前他在失事飞船兵器库里找到的独一件。这东西只能省着用，因为他们不知道如何为它补充能量，而它那道光束的秘密，在特兰技师看来至今仍是秘密。

===Original===
Ross worked his way to a curtain of underbrush from which he had a free view of the beach and the aliens. Three of them he counted, and they were Baldies, all right—taller and thinner than his own species, their bald heads gray-white, the upper dome of their skulls overshadowing the features on their pointed chinned faces. They all wore the skintight blue-purple-green suits of the space voyagers—suits which Ross knew of old were insulated and protective for their wearers, as well as a medium for keeping in touch with one another. Just as he, wearing one, had once been trailed over miles of wilderness.

To him, all three of the invaders looked enough alike to have been stamped out from one pattern. And their movements suggested that they worked or went into action with drilled precision. They all faced seaward, holding tubes aimed at the salkar-infested lagoon. There was no sound of any explosion, but green spears of light struck at the scaled bodies plunging in the water. And where those beams struck, flesh seared. Methodically the trio raked the basin. But, Ross noted, those beams which had been steady at his first sighting, were now interrupted by flickers. One of the Baldies upended his tube, rapped its butt against a rock as if trying to correct a jamming. When the alien went into action once again his weapon flashed and failed. Within a matter of moments the other two were also finished. The lighted rods pushed into the sand, giving a glow to the scene, darkened as a fire might sink to embers. Power fading?

===Chinese===
罗斯摸到一道灌木帘幕后头，从那里能把海滩和异星人看得一清二楚。他数了数，一共三个，千真万确是秃头人——比他的种族更高更瘦，光头灰白，头颅上部的穹顶压住了尖下巴面孔上的五官。三人都穿着星海旅人的蓝紫绿三色贴身服——罗斯早就领教过，那衣服隔热护身，还是穿着的人彼此保持联络的媒介。想当年他自己就套着这么一身，被人隔着好几英里的荒野追踪过。

在他眼里，三个入侵者像得如同一个模子里磕出来的。他们的举动也透着训练有素的精确，无论做事还是出手。三人都面朝大海，举着管子瞄准萨尔卡肆虐的潟湖。没有半点爆炸声，只有一道道绿色光矛刺向水里翻腾的带鳞躯体。光束落处，皮肉焦烂。三人一板一眼地扫射着湾盆。但罗斯注意到，他初见时还平稳的光束，如今已被闪烁的断续搅乱。一个秃头人把管子倒竖起来，枪托往岩石上磕，像是要排掉卡壳。那异星人再度动手时，武器闪了一下，便熄了。没过多久，另外两支也相继报销。插在沙里的发光杆原本给这一幕照明，此刻像炉火沉成余烬般暗了下去。能量在衰竭？

===Original===
An ungainly shape floundered out of the churned water, lumbered over the shale of the beach, its supple neck outstretched, its horned nose down for a gore-threatening charge. Ross had not realized that the salkars could operate out of what he thought was their natural element, but this wild-eyed dragon was plainly bent on reaching its tormentors.

For a moment or two the Baldies continued to front the creature, almost, Ross thought, as if they could not believe that their weapons had failed them. Then they broke and ran back to the fairing which they had taken with such contemptuous ease. The salkar plowed along in their wake, but its movements grew more labored the farther it advanced, until at last it lay with only its head upraised, darting it back and forth, its fanged jaws well agape, voicing a coughing howl.

Its plaint was answered from the water as a second of its kind wallowed ashore. A terrible wound had torn skin and flesh just behind its neck; yet still it came on, hissing and bubbling a battle challenge. It did not attack its fellow; instead it dragged its bulk past the first comer, on its way after the Baldies.

===Chinese===
一个笨拙的身影从翻搅的水里挣扎而出，跌跌撞撞碾过海滩的页岩，柔韧的脖颈前探，生角的口鼻压低，摆出蓄势欲顶的冲撞架势。罗斯原不曾想到萨尔卡能离开他认定的天然领域行事，可这条双眼狂乱的龙，分明铁了心要够到折磨它的仇家。

有那么一两息，秃头人们依旧直面着那家伙，罗斯觉得，他们几乎是不肯相信自己的武器竟背叛了自己。随后他们溃散了，奔回他们以不屑一顾的轻松夺下的站港。萨尔卡一路犁行，尾随其后，可它越是前进，动作越发吃力，直到最后只剩头颅昂着，来回疾啄，獠牙大张的巨口发出一阵咳呛似的嚎叫。

水里传来应和它哀鸣的声音，又一头同类翻滚着上了岸。一道可怕的伤口在它颈后撕开了皮肉；可它仍自前行，嘶嘶冒泡地发出挑战。它没有攻击同类，反而拖着庞然巨躯碾过先来者身旁，径自追秃头人去了。

===Original===
The salkars continued to come ashore, two more, a third, a fourth, mangled and torn—pulling themselves as far as they could up the beach. To lie, facing inland, their necks weaving, their horned heads bobbing, their cries a frightful din. What had drawn them out of their preoccupation of battle among themselves into this attempt to reach the aliens, Ross could not determine. Unless the intelligence of the beasts was such that they had been able to connect the searing beams which the Baldies had turned on them so tellingly with the men on the beach, and had responded by striving to reach a common enemy.

But no desire could give them the necessary energy to pull far ashore. Almost helplessly beached, they continued to dig into the yielding sand with their flippers in a vain effort to pursue the aliens.

Ross skirted the clamoring barrier of salkars and headed for the fairing. A neck snapped about; a head was lowered in his direction. He smelled the rank stench of reptile combined with burned flesh. The nearest of the brutes must have scented the Terran in turn, as it was now trying vainly to edge around to cut across Ross’s path. But it was completely outclassed on land, and the man dodged it easily.

===Chinese===
萨尔卡们继续上岸，又来两条，第三条，第四条，个个血肉模糊、遍体撕裂——拼尽全力把自己往海滩高处拖。然后躺倒，头朝内陆，脖颈摇曳，生角的头颅起伏点动，哀嚎连成一片骇人的喧嚣。是什么把它们从彼此间的酣战里拽了出来、驱向扑击异星人的这条路，罗斯断不定。除非这些巨兽的心智已高到能把秃头人狠狠灼在它们身上的光束，与滩头那些人联系在一起，认定了共同的敌人，才奋力扑来。

可再强的欲念也变不出把身子拖上滩头深处所需的力气。它们近乎无可奈何地搁浅在滩上，仍旧用鳍肢刨着松软的沙，徒劳地想追击异星人。

罗斯绕开喧腾的萨尔卡屏障，朝站港进发。一条脖颈倏地扭过来；一颗头颅朝他这边压低。他闻到爬虫的腥臊混着烧焦皮肉的恶臭。离得最近的那头畜生必定也嗅到了特兰人，此刻正徒劳地想侧身绕过来，截断罗斯的去路。可在陆地上它全然没了用武之地，那人轻巧地闪开了它。

===Original===
Three Baldies had fled this way. Yet Jazia had reported five had come out of the sea to take Kyn Add. Two were missing. Where? Had they remained in the fairing? Were they now in the sub? And that sub—what had happened to it? The broadcast had been cut off; he had seen the failure of the weapons and the shore lights. Might the sub have suffered from salkar attack? Though Ross could hardly believe that the beasts could wreck it.

The Terran was traveling blindly, keeping well under cover of such brush as he could, knowing only that he must head inland. Under his feet the ground was rising, and he recalled the nature of this territory as Torgul and Jazia had pictured it for him. This had to be part of the ridge wall of the valley in which lay the buildings of the fairing. In these heights was the Shrine of Phutka where Jazia had hidden out. To the west now lay the Rover village, so he had to work his way left, downhill, in order to reach the hole where the Baldies had gone to ground. Ross made that progress with the stealth of a trained scout.

Hawaika’s moon, triple in size to Terra’s companion, was up, and the landscape was sharply clear, with shadows well defined. The glow, weird to Terran eyes, added to the effect of being abroad in a nightmare, and the bellowing of the grounded salkars continued a devils’ chorus.

===Chinese===
三个秃头人是朝这边逃的。可贾齐娅报告过，从海里出来攻取基恩-阿德的有五个。少了两个。人在哪儿？留在站港里了？此刻正在潜艇里？还有那艘潜艇——它出了什么事？辐射中断了；武器失灵、岸上灯火熄灭，他都看在眼里。潜艇会不会也遭了萨尔卡的袭击？尽管罗斯很难相信这些巨兽能毁了它。

特兰人摸黑前行，尽量借一切可借的灌木藏身，只知道自己必须朝内陆去。脚下地势渐高，他记起托尔古尔和贾齐娅向他描绘过的这一带地貌。这里必是谷地岭墙的一段，站港的房舍便坐落在谷中。这片高地上有一座普特卡圣祠，贾齐娅当年就藏身其间。此刻掠海者的村落在西面，所以他得靠左、往低处下探，才能摸到秃头人藏身的那处巢穴。罗斯以受训斥候的潜行功夫，一步步推进。

哈瓦伊卡的月亮升在半空，足有特拉那轮的三倍大，景物清晰锋利，阴影轮廓分明。那光华在特兰人眼中透着诡谲，更添了几分行走在梦魇里的错觉，而搁浅萨尔卡的吼啸连绵不绝，唱着一支魔鬼的合唱。

===Original===
When the Rovers had put up the buildings of their fairing, they had cleared a series of small fields radiating outward from those structures. All of these were now covered with crops almost ready to harvest. The grain, if that Terran term could be applied to this Hawaikan product, was housed in long pods which dipped from shoulder-high bushes. And the pods were well equipped with horny projections which tore. A single try at making his way into one of those fields convinced Ross of the folly of such an advance. He sat back to nurse his scratched hands and survey the landscape.

To go down a very tempting lane would be making himself a clear target for anyone in those buildings ahead. He had seen the flamers of the Baldies fail on the beach, but that did not mean the aliens were now weaponless.

His best chance, Ross decided, was to circle north, come back down along the bed of a stream. And he was at the edge of that watercourse when a faint sound brought him to a frozen halt, weapon ready.

===Chinese===
掠海者建起站港房舍时，曾清理出一圈圈小田，从那片建筑向外辐射开去。如今田里全都种着即将收割的庄稼。那种谷物——倘若这个特兰字眼能安在哈瓦伊卡产物头上的话——结在长长的荚里，荚子从齐肩高的灌木上垂下来。荚壳上密生着角质的尖突，刮人。只试了一回往田里钻，罗斯便信了这条路何其愚蠢。他坐下来，揉着抓破的双手，打量四周地势。

顺一条诱人之极的小道走下去，等于把自己变成前方那些建筑里任何人的活靶子。他见过秃头人的喷焰器在海滩上失灵，可那不等于异星人如今手无寸铁。

罗斯认定，最好的机会是向北兜圈，再顺着一条溪的河床折回来。他刚到那条水道边上，一点轻微的声响便把他钉在原地，兵器就绪。

===Original===
“Rosss—”

“Loketh!”

“And Torgul and Vistur.”

This was the party from the opposite side of the island, gone expertly to earth. In the moonlight Ross could detect no sign of their presence, yet their voices sounded almost beside him.

“They are in there, in the great hall.” That was Torgul. “But no longer are there any lights.”

“Now—” An urgent exclamation drew their attention.

===Chinese===
“罗斯斯——”

“洛凯斯！”

“还有托尔古尔和维斯图尔。”

这正是从岛另一侧过来的那支队伍，行家手段地遁进了地皮。月光下罗斯探不出他们半点踪影，人声却几乎贴着他的耳边响起。

“他们在里面，在站港大厅里。”那是托尔古尔的声音。“只是里头再没有灯火。”

“快看——”一声急促的低呼引去了众人的注意。

===Original===
Light below. But not the glow of the rods Ross had seen on the beach. This was the warm yellow-red of honest fire, bursting up, the flames growing higher as if being fed with frantic haste.

Three figures were moving down there. Ross began to believe that there were only this trio ashore. He could sight no weapons in their hands, which did not necessarily mean they were unarmed. But the stream ran close behind the rear wall of one of the buildings, and Ross thought its bed could provide cover for a man who knew what he was doing. He pointed out as much to Torgul.

“And if their magic works and you are drawn out to be killed?” The Rover captain came directly to the point.

===Chinese===
下方亮起了光。但不是罗斯在海滩上见过的那种发光杆的辉光。这是真火才有的暖黄透红，轰然窜起，火苗越蹿越高，仿佛正被人发疯似的急急添柴。

下方有三条人影在动。罗斯开始相信，上岸的只有这三个人。他望不见他们手里有兵器，但这未必等于他们手无寸铁。不过那条溪恰好贴着一座建筑的后墙流过，罗斯认为，对一个懂行的人，溪床足以藏身。他把这层意思指给了托尔古尔。

“要是他们的魔法灵验，把你勾出去送死呢？”掠海者船长一针见血。

===Original===
“That is a chance to be taken. But remember … the magic of the Foanna at the sea gate did not work against me. Perhaps this won’t either. Once, earlier, I won against it.”

“Have you then another hand to give to the fire as your defense?” That was Vistur. “But no man has the right to order another’s battle challenge.”

“Just so,” returned Ross sharply. “And this is a thing I have long been trained to do.”

He slid down into the stream bed. Approaching from this angle, the structures of the fairing were between him and the fire. So screened he reached a log wall, got to his feet, and edged along it. Then he witnessed a wild scene. The fire raged in great, sky-touching tongues. And already the roof of one of the Rover buildings smoldered. Why the aliens had built up such a conflagration, Ross could not guess. A signal designed to reach some distance?

===Chinese===
“这个险非冒不可。但记住……福安娜在海门的魔法对我不起作用。说不定这个也一样。早先，我就赢过它一回。”

“那你还有另一只手，可以再交给火当护身之物吗？”那是维斯图尔。“不过，谁也没有权利命令别人应战。”

“正是如此，”罗斯干脆地回敬，“何况这是我受训多年、份内当为之事。”

他滑下溪床。从这个角度逼近，站港的建筑正好隔在他与火堆之间。借着这重遮蔽，他摸到一面原木墙，起身，贴墙挪行。随后他目睹了一幕狂乱的景象。大火烈焰翻卷，直舔天际。一座掠海者建筑的屋顶已经冒起烟来。异星人为何堆起这样一场大火，罗斯猜不透。是为了传到远方的信号？

===Original===
He did not doubt there was some urgent purpose. For the three were dragging in fuel with almost frenzied haste, bringing out of the Rover buildings bales of cloth to be ripped apart and whirled into the devouring flames, furniture, everything movable which would burn.

There was one satisfaction. The Baldies were so intent upon this destruction that they kept no watch save that now and then one of them would run to the head of the path leading to the lagoon and listen as if he expected a salkar to come pounding up the slope.

“They’re … they’re rattled!” Ross could hardly believe it. The Baldies who had always occupied his mind and memory as practically invincible supermen were acting like badly frightened primitives! And when the enemy was so off balance you pushed—you pushed hard.

===Chinese===
他不怀疑这里头有紧迫的目的。因为那三人正近乎发狂地急着拖燃料，从掠海者的建筑里搬出一捆捆布匹，撕碎了抡进贪婪的烈火，还有家具，一切搬得动又烧得着的东西。

唯有一件事令人快意。秃头人们一心扑在这场破坏上，全不设岗守望，只是偶尔有一人跑到通往潟湖的小径尽头去听动静，仿佛在提防哪条萨尔卡轰隆隆冲上坡来。

“他们……他们慌神了！”罗斯几乎不敢相信。在他心里和记忆里一向约等于无敌超人的秃头人，此刻的做派竟像吓破了胆的蛮子！敌人阵脚一乱，你就得推——狠狠地推。

===Original===
Ross thumbed the button on the grip of the strange weapon. He sighted with deliberation and fired. The blue figure at the top of the path wilted, and for a long moment neither of his companions noted his collapse. Then one of them whirled and started for the limp body, his colleague running after him. Ross allowed them to reach his first victim before he fired the second and third time.

All three lay quiet, but still Ross did not venture forth until he had counted off a dozen Terran seconds. Then he slipped forward keeping to cover until he came up to the bodies.

The blue-clad shoulder had a flaccid feel under his hand as if the muscles could not control the flesh about them. Ross rolled the alien over, looked down in the bright light of the fire into the Baldy’s wide-open eyes. Amazement—the Terran thought he could read that in the dead stare which answered his intent gaze—and then anger, a cold and deadly anger which chilled into ice.

===Chinese===
罗斯拇指按上那件奇异兵器的握把按钮。他从容瞄准，开火。小径尽头那条蓝影蔫软下去，好一阵子，他的两个同伴谁也没察觉他倒了。随后其中一人旋身奔向那瘫软的躯体，同伙紧追而去。罗斯放他们到了第一个牺牲品跟前，才开了第二枪和第三枪。

三个都静静地躺着，可罗斯仍按兵不动，默数了特兰式的十二秒。然后他才悄然前出，借掩体一路潜行，直到摸到那三具躯体跟前。

掌下的蓝衣肩膀松软塌陷，仿佛肌肉已号令不动包裹其间的血肉。罗斯把那异星人翻过来，就着明亮的火光，低头望进秃头人大睁的双眼。惊愕——特兰人觉得自己能从那回敬他凝视的呆滞目光里读出这个——随后是怒，一种冰冷致命、寒到凝成坚冰的怒意。

===Original===
“Kill!”

Ross slewed around, still down on one knee, to face the charge of a Rover. In the firelight the Hawaikan’s eyes were blazing with fanatical hatred. He had his hooked sword ready to deliver a finishing stroke. The Terran blocked with a shoulder to meet the Rover’s knees, threw him back. Then Ross landed on top of the fighting crewman, trying to pin the fellow to earth and avoid that recklessly slashing blade.

“Loketh! Vistur!” Ross shouted as he struggled.

More of the Rovers appeared from between the buildings, bearing down on the limp aliens and the two fighting men. Ross recognized the limping gait of Loketh using a branch to aid him into a running scuttle across the open.

“Loketh—here!”

The Hawaikan covered the last few feet in a dive which carried him into Ross and the Rover. “Hold him,” the Terran ordered and had just time enough to throw himself between the Baldies and the rest of the crew. There was a snarling from the Rovers; and Ross, knowing their temper, was afraid he could not save the captives which they considered, fairly, their legitimate prey. He must depend upon the hope that there were one or two cooler heads among them with enough authority to restrain the would-be avengers. Otherwise he would have to beam them into helplessness.

===Chinese===
“杀！”

罗斯单膝犹屈，霍地转身，迎上一个掠海者的冲锋。火光里，那哈瓦伊卡人的双眼燃烧着狂热的仇恨。他弯刀已然举起，就要补上致命一击。特兰人用肩膀迎向对方的膝盖，把他掀了回去。接着罗斯压上这个厮打不休的船员，一边竭力把人摁在地上，一边躲着那把不管不顾乱劈的刀。

“洛凯斯！维斯图尔！”罗斯边搏斗边吼。

更多掠海者从建筑间涌出，直扑那几个瘫软的异星人和缠斗的两人。罗斯认出了洛凯斯那跛行的步态——他拄着一根树枝，撑着自己疾窜着越过开阔地。

“洛凯斯——这边！”

那哈瓦伊卡人一个鱼跃扑完最后几英尺，连人带势撞进罗斯和那掠海者中间。“摁住他。”特兰人下令，随即抢身，堪堪来得及把自己挡在秃头人与其余船员之间。掠海者中响起一片咆哮；罗斯深知他们的火性，生怕保不住那几个俘虏——在他们眼里，那本就是名正言顺属于他们的猎物。他只能指望人群里有一两个头脑冷静、又有足够威望的人，能约束住这些一心复仇的人。否则他就只好用光束把他们也撂倒。

===Original===
“Torgul!” he shouted.

There was a break in the line of runners speeding for him. The big man lunging straight across could only be Vistur; the other, yelling orders, was Torgul. It would depend upon how much control the Captain had over his men. Ross scrambled to his feet. He had clicked on the beamer to its lowest frequency. It would not kill, but would render its victim temporarily paralyzed; and how long that state would continue Ross had no way of knowing. Tried on Terran laboratory animals, the time had varied from days to weeks.

Vistur used the flat side of his war ax, clapping it against the foremost runners, setting his own bulk to impose a barrier. And now Torgul’s orders appeared to be getting through, more and more of the men slacked, leaving a trio of hotheads, two of whom Vistur sent reeling with his fists.

===Chinese===
“托尔古尔！”他吼道。

朝他猛冲的人链上破开了一道口子。径直横撞过去的那个大块头只能是维斯图尔；另一个扬声喝令的，是托尔古尔。成败就看船长对部下还有几分约束力了。罗斯一跃而起。他已把光束枪（beamer）拨到最低频率。这一挡不取性命，却能让中招者暂时瘫痪；这状态会持续多久，罗斯无从知晓。在特兰实验动物身上试过，短则数日，长则数周。

维斯图尔抡起战斧的斧背，拍向冲在最前的几人，拿自己的身躯横成一道屏障。此刻托尔古尔的号令似乎见了效，越来越多的人放缓脚步，只剩三个头脑发热的还往前闯，其中两个被维斯图尔几拳打得踉跄倒退。

===Original===
The Captain came up to Ross. “They are alive then?” He leaned over to inspect the Baldy the Terran had rolled on his back, assessing the alien’s frozen stare with thoughtful measurement.

“Yes, but they can not move.”

“Well enough.” Torgul nodded. “They shall meet the Justice of Phutka after the Law. I think they will wish that they had been left to the boarding axes of angry men.”

“They are worth more alive than dead, Captain. Do you not wish to know why they have carried war to your people, how many of them there may yet be to attack—and other things? Also—” Ross nodded at the fire now catching the second building, “why have they built up that blaze? Is it a signal to others of their kind?”

===Chinese===
船长走到罗斯跟前。“那么，他们还活着？”他俯身察看那具被特兰人翻成仰面的秃头人，掂量着那异星人凝滞的目光，若有所思。

“活着，只是动弹不得。”

“那就好。”托尔古尔点头。“他们将依律受普特卡的审判。我想，到时候他们宁愿当初就被愤怒之人的接舷斧了断。”

“他们活着比死了值钱，船长。你难道不想知道他们为什么把战火引到你的族人头上、还会有多少人马前来进攻——还有旁的事？另外——”罗斯朝此刻已烧着第二座建筑的大火点点头，“他们为什么堆起这场大火？是不是打给他们同类的信号？”

===Original===
“Very well said. Yes, it would be well for us to learn such things. Nor will Phutka be jealous of the time we take to ask questions and get answers, many answers.” He prodded the Baldy with the toe of his sea boot.

“How long will they remain so? Your magic has a bite in it.”

Ross smiled. “Not my magic, Captain. This weapon was taken from one of their own ships. As to how long they will remain so—that I do not know.”

===Chinese===
“说得在理。是啊，这些事我们弄明白了好。普特卡也不会见怪我们多花些工夫，问出口供，问出许许多多答案。”他用海上靴的靴尖戳了戳那秃头人。

“他们这副样子会维持多久？你那魔法咬人得很。”

罗斯微微一笑。“这不是我的魔法，船长。这件兵器是从他们自己人的一艘船上取来的。至于他们会这样多久——那我不知道。”

===Original===
“Very well, we can take precautions.” Under Torgul’s orders the aliens were draped with capture nets like those Ross and Loketh had worn. The sea-grown plant adhered instantly, wet strands knitting in perfect restrainers as long as it was uncut.

Having seen to that, Torgul ordered the excavation of Kyn Add.

“As you say,” he remarked to Ross, “that fire may well be a signal to bring down more of their kind. I think we have had the Favor of Phutka in this matter, but the prudent man stretches no favor of that kind too far. Also,” he looked about him—“we have given to Phutka and the Shades our dead; there is nothing for us here now but hate and sorrow. In one day we have been broken from a clan of pride and ships to a handful of standardless men.”

===Chinese===
“很好，那我们可以早作防备。”托尔古尔一声令下，几个异星人被罩上了捕网——就是罗斯和洛凯斯当初穿戴过的那种。那海生植物一搭上身子便即刻贴附，湿淋淋的网丝绞结成天衣无缝的束缚，只要不剪断，便一直有效。

料理完这一桩，托尔古尔下令搜掘基恩-阿德。

“正如你所说，”他对罗斯道，“那场火很可能是个信号，会招来更多他们的同类。我想这件事里我们已得了普特卡的眷顾，可谨慎之人不会把这种眷顾逼得太狠。还有，”他环顾四周——“我们已把死者交给了普特卡与众神灵；此刻此地留给我们的，只剩仇恨与悲哀。一天之间，我们从一个拥有骄傲与船舰的氏族，散成了一小撮无旗之人。”

===Original===
“You will join some other clan?” Karara had come with Jazia to stand on the stone ledge chipped to form a base for a column bearing a strange, brooding-eyed head looking seaward. The Rover woman was superintending the freeing of the head from the column.

At the Terran girl’s question the Captain gazed down into the dreadful chaos of the valley. They could yet hear the roars of the dying salkars. The reptiles that had made their way to land had not withdrawn but still lay, some dead now, some with weaving heads reaching inland. And the whole of the fairing was ablaze with fire.

“We are now blood-sworn men, Sea Maid. For such there is no clan. There is only the hunting and the kill. With the magic of Phutka perhaps we shall have a short hunt and a good kill.”

“There … now … so. …” Jazia stepped back. The head which had faced the sea was lowered carefully to a wide strip of crimson-and-gold stuff she had brought from Torgul’s ship. With her one usable hand the Rover woman drew the fabric about the carving, muffling it except for the eyes. Those were large ovals deeply carved, and in them Ross saw a glitter. Jewels set there? Yet, he had a queer, shivery feeling that something more than gems occupied those sockets—that he had actually been regarded for an instant of time, assessed and dismissed.

===Chinese===
“你们会去投别的氏族吗？”卡拉拉随贾齐娅一道来了，站在一方凿出的石台基上——台基上原立着一根柱子，顶着颗奇异的石首，一双沉郁的眼睛望向大海。那位掠海者妇人正督着人把石首从柱上卸下来。

听了特兰姑娘这一问，船长俯瞰谷中那可怕的劫乱。垂死的萨尔卡的吼声仍隐约可闻。上了岸的爬虫们没有退走，仍旧躺着，有的已经死了，有的还摇着脖颈朝内陆探伸。整座站港陷在一片火海之中。

“我们如今是歃血立誓之人了，海之少女。这样的人不再有氏族。有的只是狩猎与诛杀。有普特卡的神力相助，或许我们会有一场短短的狩猎、一次漂亮的诛杀。”

“好了……这下……成了。……”贾齐娅退后一步。那颗面朝大海的石首被小心翼翼地放落到一条红金相间的宽幅织料上，那是她从托尔古尔船上取来的。那位掠海者妇人用她仅剩的可用之手，把织物裹上雕像，只露出双眼。那是两只深深镌刻的大椭圆，罗斯看见里面有微光闪动。是镶在那里的宝石？然而他有一种奇异的、令脊背发凉的感觉：占据那两个眼窝的不止是宝石——有一瞬间，那东西确确实实打量过他，掂量过他，又弃之不顾。

===Original===
“We go now.” Jazia waved and Torgul sent men forward. They lifted the wrapped carving to a board carried between them and started downslope.

Karara cried out and Ross looked around.

The pillar which had supported the head was crumbling away, breaking into a rubble which cascaded across the stone ledge. Ross blinked—this must be an illusion, but he was too tired to be more than dully amazed as he became one of the procession returning to the ships.

===Chinese===
“我们这就走。”贾齐娅一挥手，托尔古尔遣人上前。众人抬起裹好的雕像，放上一条两人合抬的木板，动身下坡。

卡拉拉惊呼出声，罗斯循声望去。

承托石首的柱子正在崩解，碎成一摊摊碎石，泻过石台基。罗斯眨了眨眼——这必定是错觉，可他实在疲惫不堪，只余一阵木然的惊异，便这样汇入了返回船队的行列。

## XIII The Sea Gate of the Foanna / XIII 福安娜的海门

===Original===
Ross raised a shell cup to his lips but hardly sipped the fiery brew it contained. This was a gesture of ceremony, but he wanted a steady head and a quick tongue for any coming argument. Torgul, Afrukta, Ongal﻿—the three commanders of the Rover cruisers; Jazia, who represented the mysterious Power of Phutka; Vistur and some other subordinate officers; Karara; himself, with Loketh hovering behind: a council of war. But summoned against whom?

The Terran had come too far afield from his own purpose﻿—to reach Ashe in the Foanna keep. And to further his own plans was a task he doubted his ability to perform. His attack on the Baldies had made him too important to the Rovers for them to allow him willingly to leave them on a quest of his own.

“These star men”﻿—Ross set down the cup, tried to choose the most telling words in his limited Hawaikan vocabulary﻿—“possess weapons and powers you can not dream of, that you have no defense against. Back at Kyn Add we were lucky. The salkars attacked their sub and halted the broadcast powering their flamers. Otherwise we could not have taken them, even though we were many against their few. Now you talk of hunting them in their own territory﻿—on land and in the mountains where they have their base. That would be folly akin to swimming barehanded to front a salkar.”

===Chinese===
罗斯举起一只贝壳杯凑到唇边，却几乎没有啜饮杯中那火辣的酒浆。这只是礼仪之举，因为无论即将来临的争论如何，他都需要清醒的头脑和伶俐的口舌。托尔古尔、阿夫鲁克塔、翁加尔——掠海者巡航舰的三位司令；代表神秘普特卡神力的贾齐娅；维斯图尔和其他几名下级军官；卡拉拉；还有他自己，洛凯斯侍立在他身后：这是一场军务会议。可它是为了对付谁而召集的呢？

这个特兰人已经偏离自己的目标太远——他的目标，是到福安娜的要塞去找到阿什。而要推进自己的计划，实在是他怀疑自己有没有能力完成的任务。他袭击秃头人一事，让他在掠海者心中变得太重要了，他们绝不肯心甘情愿地放他离开队伍去追寻自己的目标。

「这些星人」——罗斯放下杯子，尽力从自己有限的哈瓦伊卡语词汇里挑出最有分量的话——「拥有你们做梦也想不到的武器和力量，你们根本无从防御。在基恩-阿德那时我们是走运。巨鲨攻击了他们的潜艇，中断了给他们喷焰器供能的广播波。否则我们根本抓不到他们，哪怕我们人多、他们人少。现在你们却谈论要到他们自己的地盘上去猎杀他们——上陆地、进山里，他们的基地就设在那里。那种行为愚蠢得就像赤手空拳游去迎击一头巨鲨。」

===Original===
“So﻿—then we must sit and wait for them to eat us up?” flared Ongal. “I say it is better to die fighting with one’s blade wet!”

“Do you not also wish to take at least one of the enemy with you when you fight to that finish?” Ross countered. “These could kill you before you came in blade range.”

“You had no trouble with that weapon of yours,” Afrukta spoke up.

“I have told you﻿—this weapon was stolen from them. I have only one and I do not know how long it will continue to serve me, or whether they have a defense against it. Those we took were naked to any force, for their broadcast had failed them. But to smash blindly against their main base would be the act of madmen.”

===Chinese===
「所以——我们就得干坐着等他们把我们一个个吞掉？」翁加尔勃然大怒。「我说，与其那样，不如血染刀锋、奋战而死！」

「你们战至最后一刻时，难道不想至少拉上一个敌人垫背吗？」罗斯反问。「这些人却能在你进入刀刃够得着的距离之前就要了你的命。」

「你那件武器用起来不是很顺手吗。」阿夫鲁克塔开了口。

「我说过——这件武器是从他们手里缴获的。我只有一件，而且不知道它还能为我效命多久，也不知道他们有没有对付它的手段。我们抓获的那些人之所以毫无抵抗之力，是因为他们的供能广播波失灵了。可要是不管不顾地去硬撞他们的主基地，那是疯子才干得出来的事。」

===Original===
“The salkars opened a way for us﻿—” That was Torgul.

“But we can not move a pack of those inland to the mountains,” Vistur pointed out reasonably.

Ross studied the Captain. That Torgul was groping for a plan and that it had to be a shrewd one, the Terran guessed. His respect for the Rover commander had been growing steadily since their first meeting. The cruiser-raiders had always been captained by the most daring men of the Rover clans. But Ross was also certain that a successful cruiser commander must possess a levelheaded leaven of intelligence and be a strategist of parts.

The Hawaikan force needed a key which would open the Baldy base as the salkars had opened the lagoon. And all they had to aid them was a handful of facts gained from their prisoners.

===Chinese===
「是巨鲨为我们打开了通路——」说话的是托尔古尔。

「可我们没办法把一群巨鲨搬过陆地、运进山里啊。」维斯图尔有理有据地指出。

罗斯打量着这位舰长。特兰人猜得到，托尔古尔正在摸索一个方案，而且这方案必须极为精明。自初次见面以来，他对这位掠海者指挥官的敬意一直在稳步增长。出海劫掠的巡航舰向来由掠海者各氏族中最胆大的人统率。但罗斯同样确信，一个屡屡得手的巡航舰指挥官必须兼备冷静清醒的头脑和过人的谋略，得是个有两下子的战略家。

哈瓦伊卡人的队伍需要一把钥匙，一把能像巨鲨攻破礁湖那样撬开秃头人基地的钥匙。而他们手里能派上用场的，只有从俘虏身上得到的一丁点情报。

===Original===
Oddly enough the picklock to the captives’ minds had been produced by the dolphins. Just as Tino-rau and Taua had formed a bridge of communication between the Terran and Loketh, so did they read and translate the thoughts of the galactic invaders. For the Baldies, among their own kind, were telepathic, vocalizing only to give orders to inferiors.

Their capture by these primitive “inferiors” had delivered the first shock, and the mind-probes of the dolphins had sent the “supermen” close to the edge of sanity. To accept an animal form as an equal had been shattering.

But the star men’s thoughts and memories had been winnowed at last and the result spread before this impromptu council. Rovers and Terrans were briefed on the invaders’ master plan for taking over a world. Why they desired to do so even the dolphins had not been able to discover; perhaps they themselves had not been told by their superiors.

It was a plan almost contemptuous in its simplicity, as if the galactic force had no reason to fear effective opposition. Except in one direction﻿—one single direction.

===Chinese===
说来奇怪，撬开俘虏心灵之锁的「开锁匠」竟是海豚。正如蒂诺-劳和塔乌亚曾在特兰人与洛凯斯之间架起沟通的桥梁一样，它们也读取并转译了这些银河入侵者的想法。因为秃头人与自己同类交流靠的是心灵感应，只有向下级下达命令时才开口出声。

被这些原始的「下等生物」俘获已经给了他们第一重打击，而海豚的心灵探针更把这些「超人」逼到了精神崩溃的边缘。要他们承认一种兽形生物与自己平起平坐，简直五雷轰顶。

不过，星人的思想和记忆最终被细细筛检了一遍，结果摊在了这场临时军务会议的面前。掠海者和特兰人由此得知了入侵者接管一个世界的总体规划。至于他们为什么想这么做，连海豚都没能探出来；也许连他们自己也没有从上级那里得到过解释。

那计划简单得近乎傲慢，仿佛这支银河部队压根不认为有理由害怕任何有效的抵抗。除了一个方向——唯一的一个方向。

===Original===
Ross’s fingers tightened on the shell cup. Had Torgul reached that conclusion yet, the belief that the Foanna could be their key? If so, they might be able to achieve their separate purposes in one action.

“It would seem that they are wary of the Foanna,” he suggested, alert to any telltale response from Torgul. But it was Jazia who answered the Terran’s half question.

“The Foanna have a powerful magic; they can order wind and wave, man and creature﻿—if so be their will. Well might these killers fear the Foanna!”

“Yet now they move against them,” Ross pointed out, still eyeing Torgul.

===Chinese===
罗斯的手指捏紧了贝壳杯。托尔古尔得出那个结论了吗——也就是相信福安娜可以成为他们的钥匙？如果是这样，他们或许能靠一次行动各自达成目的。

「看来他们对福安娜有所忌惮。」他试探道，留神捕捉托尔古尔任何露底的反应。但接过特兰人这半个问话的却是贾齐娅。

「福安娜拥有强大的魔法；只要他们愿意，就能号令风与浪、人与兽。这些杀人凶手会怕福安娜，理所当然！」

「可现在他们要对福安娜下手了。」罗斯指出，目光仍盯着托尔古尔。

===Original===
The Captain’s reply was a small, quiet smile.

“Not directly, as you have heard. It is all a part of their plan to set one of us against the other, letting us fight many small wars and so use up our men while they take no risks. They wait the day when we shall be exhausted and then they will reveal themselves to claim all they wish. So today they stir up trouble between the Wreckers and the Foanna, knowing that the Foanna are few. Also they strive in turn to anger us by raids, allowing us to believe that either the Wreckers or Foanna have attacked. Thus﻿—” he held up his left thumb, made a pincers of right thumb and forefinger to close upon it, “they hope to catch the Foanna, between Wreckers and Rovers. Because the Foanna are those they reckon the most dangerous they move against them now, using us and weakening our forces into the bargain. A plan which is clever, but the plan of men who do not like to fight with their own blades.”

“They are worse than the coast scum, these cowards!” Ongal spat.

Torgul smiled again. “That is what they believe we will say, kinsman, and so underrate them. By our customs, yes, they are cowards. But what care they for our judgments? Did we think of the salkars when we used them to force the lagoon? No, they were only beasts to be our tools. So now it is the same with us, except that we know what they intend. And we shall not be such obedient tools. If the Foanna are our answer, then﻿—” He paused, gazing into his cup as if he could read some shadowy future there.

===Chinese===
舰长的回答只是静静的一丝浅笑。

「不是正面下手，你也听到了。他们的全盘计划就是让我们自相残杀，让我们打许多场小仗，耗光我们的人手，而他们自己毫发无险。他们等着我们筋疲力尽的那一天，再到亮出真面目，索要他们想要的一切。所以今天他们在海难匪和福安娜之间挑起事端，因为他们知道福安娜人少。他们又轮番袭掠来激怒我们，让我们以为袭击出自海难匪或福安娜之手。这样——」他竖起左手拇指，用右手拇指和食指圈成一个钳形夹住它，「他们就想把福安娜夹在海难匪和掠海者中间。正因为福安娜是他们眼中最大的威胁，他们现在才对福安娜动手，借我们之手行事，还顺带消耗我们的兵力。这计划确实高明，却是那种不肯亲自拔剑之人的计划。」

「这些懦夫，比海岸上的渣滓还不如！」翁加尔啐了一口。

托尔古尔又笑了。「亲族，这正是他们料定我们会说的话，而我们也就因此小看了他们。按我们的规矩，他们确实是懦夫。可他们在乎我们的评判吗？我们驱使巨鲨攻破礁湖的时候，可曾把巨鲨放在眼里？没有，它们不过是供我们驱使的畜生。如今轮到我们，处境一样——只除了我们已经知道他们的图谋。而我们不会做那么听话的工具。如果福安娜就是我们的答案，那么——」他顿住了，凝视着杯中，仿佛能从里面读出某种朦胧的未来。

===Original===
“If the Foanna are the answer, then what?” Ross pushed.

“Instead of fighting the Foanna, we must warm, cherish, try to ally ourselves with them. And do all that while we still have time!”

“Just how do we do these things?” demanded Ongal. “The Foanna you would warn, cherish, claim as allies, are already our enemies. Were we not on the way to force their sea gate only days ago? There is no chance of seeking peace now. And have the finned ones not learned from the women-killers that already there is an army of Wreckers camped about the citadel to which these sons of the Shadow plan to lend certain weapons? Do we throw away three cruisers﻿—all we have left﻿—in a hopeless fight? Such is the council of one struck by loss of wits.”

===Chinese===
「如果福安娜是答案，那又怎样？」罗斯追问。

「与其同福安娜为敌，我们必须去温暖他们、珍视他们，设法与他们结盟。而且要趁我们还来得及的时候把这一切做成！」

「那这些事究竟怎么办得到？」翁加尔质问道。「你要去警示、去珍视、要结为盟友的福安娜，早就是我们的敌人了。几天前我们不正是在开往他们海门的路上吗？现在再求和平，绝无可能。再说，有鳍的伙伴们不是已从那些杀女人的凶手口中得知，有一支海难匪大军正围着城塞扎营，而这些阴影之子还打算给他们送去某些武器？我们要把仅剩的三艘巡航舰——我们的全部家当——扔进一场毫无希望的战斗里吗？这是失心疯才想得出的主意。」

===Original===
“There is a way﻿—my way,” Ross seized the opening. “In the Foanna citadel is my sword-lord, to whose service I am vowed. We were on our way to attempt his freeing when your ship picked us out of the waves. He is learned beyond me in the dealing with strange peoples, and if the Foanna are as clever as you say, they will already have discovered that he is not just a slave they claimed from Lord Zahur.”

There it was in the open, his own somewhat tattered hope that Ashe had been able to impress his captors with his knowledge and potential. Trained to act as contact man with other races, there was a chance that Gordon had saved himself from whatever fate had been planned for the prisoners the Foanna had claimed. If that happened, Ashe could be their opening wedge in the Foanna stronghold.

“This also I know: That which guards the gate﻿—which turns your minds whirling and sent you back from your raid﻿—does not affect me. I may be able to win inside and find my clansman, and in that doing treat with the Foanna.”

===Chinese===
「有一个办法——我的办法。」罗斯抓住这个话头。「福安娜的城塞里关着我的剑主，我立誓效忠于他。你们的船从浪里把我们救起时，我们正要去设法救他。在与陌生种族打交道上，他比我有学问；而如果福安娜真像你们说的那么精明，他们早该发现他绝不是一介从扎胡尔领主手里分来的普通奴隶。」

话已挑明——他自己那点已经有些破烂的希望：阿什凭他的学识和潜质折服了俘获他的人。戈登受过专门训练，惯于充当与其他种族联络的中间人，福安娜分走的俘虏本来等着某种命运，他有机会让自己躲过那一劫。若真如此，阿什就能成为他们楔入福安娜堡垒的破城锥。

「我还知道一件事：守卫海门的那东西——那让你们头晕目眩、逼得你们空手而回的东西——对我没有影响。我也许能进得去，找到我的族人，并借此与福安娜谈判。」

===Original===
The Baldy prisoners had not underestimated the attack on the Foanna citadel. As the Rover cruisers beat in under the cover of night the fires and torches of both besieged and besiegers made a wild glow across the sky. Only on the sea side of the fortress there was no sign of involvement. Whatever guarded the gate must still be in force.

Ross stood with his feet well apart to balance his body against the swing of the deck. His suggestion had been argued over, protested, but at last carried with the support of Torgul and Jazia, and now he was to make his try. The sum of the Rovers’ and Loketh’s knowledge of the sea gate had been added for his benefit, but he knew that this venture must depend upon himself alone. Karara, the dolphins, the Hawaikans, were all too sensitive to the barrier.

Torgul moved in the faint light. “We are close; our power is ebbing. If we advance, we shall be drifting soon.”

“It is time then.” Ross crossed to the rope ladder, but another was there before him. Karara perched on the rail. He regarded her angrily.

===Chinese===
秃头人俘虏们对进攻福安娜城塞的估计并没有夸大。当掠海者的巡航舰趁夜色驶进时，围城者和守城者双方的火光与火炬把天空映得一片狂乱。只有堡垒临海的一侧毫无交战迹象。守卫海门的那个东西，威力必然仍在。

罗斯叉开双腿站稳，抵住甲板的摇晃。他的提议经过反复争辩和反对，最终在托尔古尔和贾齐娅的支持下通过了，现在就要由他来一试身手。为了他此行，掠海者与洛凯斯关于海门的全部知识都已倾囊相授，但他清楚，这次冒险只能靠他自己一个人。卡拉拉、海豚、还有哈瓦伊卡人，对那道屏障都太过敏感。

托尔古尔在微光中动了动。「我们已经很近了；我们的动力正在衰减。再往前，很快就只能随波漂流了。」

「那么是时候了。」罗斯走向绳梯，却有人抢先一步到了那里。卡拉拉坐在栏杆上。他气冲冲地瞪着她。

===Original===
“You can’t go.”

“I know. But we are still safe here. Just because you are free of one defense of the gate, Ross, do not believe that makes it easy.”

He was stung by her assumption that he could be so self-assured.

“I know my business.”

Ross pushed past her, swinging down the rope ladder, pausing only above water level to snap on flippers, make sure of the set of his weighted belt, and slide his gill-mask over his face. There was a splash beside him as the net containing spare belt, flippers, and mask hit the water and he caught at it. These could provide Ashe’s escape from the fortress.

===Chinese===
「你不能去。」

「我知道。可我们在这里还算安全。罗斯，别以为你不受海门一道防御的影响，这事就轻而易举了。」

她竟以为他会如此自信满满，这话刺痛了他。

「我清楚自己在干什么。」

罗斯从她身边挤过去，顺着绳梯往下荡，只在贴近水面的地方停了停，扣好脚蹼，确认配重腰带系得妥帖，再把鳃式面罩罩上脸。旁边哗啦一响，装着备用腰带、脚蹼和面罩的网兜落进水里，他一把抓了过来。有了这些，阿什就能逃出堡垒。

===Original===
The lights on the shore made a wide arc of radiance across the sea. As Ross headed toward the wave-washed coast he began to hear shouting and other sounds which made him believe that the besiegers were in the midst of an all-out assault. Yet those distant fires and rocketlike blasts into the sky had a wavery blur. And Ross, making his way with the effortless water cleaving of the diver, surfaced now and then to spot film curling up from the surface of the sea between the two standing rock pillars which marked the sea gate.

He was startled by a thunderous crack, rending the air above the small bay. Ross pulled to one of the pillars, steadied himself with one hand against it. Those twists of film rising from the surging surface were thickening. More tendrils grew out from parent stems to creep along above the waves, raising up sprouts and branches in turn. A wall of mist was building between gate and shore.

Again a thunderclap overhead. Involuntarily the Terran ducked. Then he turned his face up to the sky, striving to see any evidence of storm. What hung there sped the growth of the fog on the water. Yet where the fog was gray-white, it was a darkness spouting from the highest point of the citadel. Ross could not explain how he was able to see one shade of darkness against equal dusk, but he did﻿—or did he only sense it? He shook his head, willing himself to look away from the finger. Only it was a finger no longer; now it was a fist aimed at the stars it was fast blotting out. A fist rising to the heavens before it curled back, descended to press the fortress and its surroundings into rock and earth.

===Chinese===
岸上的灯火在海面划出一道宽阔的光弧。罗斯朝着被浪冲刷的海岸进发，途中渐渐听见喊杀声和其他动静，让他相信围城者正在发起全面强攻。然而那些远处的火光和火箭般射向天空的爆破都带着一层摇曳的模糊。罗斯以潜水者劈波斩水的从容姿态一路前行，时时浮上水面，看见两根标示海门的立岩之间，海面上正有薄雾袅袅升起。

一声霹雳般的巨响炸开，撕裂了小海湾上空的空气，吓了罗斯一跳。他游到其中一根立岩边，一只手扶住它稳住身形。那些从翻涌海面升起的雾缕正在变浓。更多的雾须从主干上生出来，贴着浪头蔓延，又陆续抽出枝杈。一道雾墙正在海门与海岸之间筑起。

头顶又是一声炸雷。特兰人不由自主地缩了一下头。随后他仰起脸望天，想找出风暴来临的迹象。悬在天上的那东西正催促水面的雾加速生长。可雾是灰白色的，那东西却是从城塞最高点喷涌而出的一团黑暗。罗斯说不清自己怎么能在一色暮昏里分辨出这一层更深的暗，可他确实看见了——或者只是感觉到了？他摇摇头，强迫自己把目光从那根手指上挪开。只是它已不再是一根手指；此刻它是一只拳头，直指它正迅速遮蔽的群星。那拳头向天穹高举，随即蜷回、下压，仿佛要把堡垒连同四周碾进岩石与泥土之中。

===Original===
Fog curled about Ross, spilled outward through the sea gates. He loosed his grip on the pillar and dived, swimming on through the gap with the fortress of the Foanna before him.

There was a jetty somewhere ahead; that much he knew from Torgul’s description. Those who served the Foanna sometimes took sea roads and they had slim, fast cutters for such coastwise travel. Ross surfaced cautiously, to discover there was no visibility to wave level. Here the mist was thick, a smothering cover so bewildering he was confused as to direction. He ducked below again and flippered on.

Was his confusion born of the fog, or was it also in his head? Did he, after all, have this much reaction to the gate defense? Ross ducked that suspicion as he had ducked the moist blanket on the surface. He had come from the gate, which meant that the jetty must lie﻿—there!

===Chinese===
浓雾在罗斯周身盘旋，又从海门向外倾泻。他松开抓着立岩的手，一个猛子扎下去，穿过缺口继续向前游，福安娜的堡垒就在前方。

前方某处有一座栈桥——这是他从托尔古尔的描述中得知的。侍奉福安娜的人有时走海路，他们备有细长快速的快艇，专门用于近岸航行。罗斯小心翼翼浮上水面，却发现连浪尖都望不出去。这里雾浓得像一床闷人的厚被，搅得他晕头转向、辨不清方向。他又潜回水下，划着脚蹼继续前进。

他的迷乱是雾造成的，还是出自他自己的头脑？难道他终究还是对海门的防御起了那么一点反应？罗斯像躲开水面那床湿被一样躲开了这个念头。他是从海门游过来的，那么栈桥必定在——那里！

===Original===
A few moments later Ross had proof that his sense of direction had not altogether failed him, when his shoulder grazed against a solid obstruction in the water and his exploring touch told him that he had found one of the jetty piles. He surfaced again and this time he heard not a thunder roll but the singsong chanting of the Foanna.

It was loud, almost directly above his head, but since the cotton mist held he was not afraid of being sighted. The chanter must be on the jetty. And to Ross’s right was a dark bulk which he thought was one of the cutters. Was a sortie by the besieged being planned?

Then, out of the night, came a dazzling beam, well above the level of Ross’s head where he clung to the piling. It centered on the cutter, slicing into the substance of the vessel with the ease of steel piercing clay. The chanting stopped on mid-note, broken by cries of surprise and alarm. Ross, pressing against the pile, received a jolt from his belt sonic.

There must be a Baldy sub in the basin inside the gate. Perhaps the flame beam now destroying the cutter was to be turned on the walls of the keep in turn.

===Chinese===
片刻之后，罗斯的肩膀擦到水中一处坚实的障碍，探摸的手告诉他，那是栈桥的桩柱——他的方向感总算没有全然失灵。他再次浮出水面，这一次听到的不是滚滚雷声，而是福安娜抑扬顿挫的吟唱。

吟唱声很响，几乎就在他头顶正上方，但棉絮般的浓雾仍未散去，他不怕被人看见。吟唱者必定在栈桥上。罗斯右手边伏着一团黑影，他想那是其中一条快艇。守城者是在策划一次突围吗？

接着，夜幕中射来一道刺目的光束，远远高过罗斯抱住桩柱处的头顶位置。光束锁定那条快艇，切入船体的轻松劲儿，就像钢刀捅进黏土。吟唱在半个音节上戛然而止，惊呼与警报声随之炸开。罗斯紧贴着桩柱，腰带上的声波器猛地震了一下。

门内的盆湾水域里必定藏着一艘秃头人的潜艇。此刻正毁灭快艇的那道喷焰光束，说不定接下来就会转向要塞的墙壁。

===Original===
Foanna chant again, low and clear. Splashes from the water as those on the jetty cast into the sea objects Ross could not define. The Terran’s body jerked, his mask smothered a cry of pain. About his legs and middle, immersed in the waves, there was cold so intense that it seared. Fear goaded him to pull up on one of the under beams of the pier. He reached that refuge and rubbed his icy legs with what vigor he could summon.

Moments later he crept along toward the shore. The energy ray had found another target. Ross paused to watch a second cutter sliced. If the counter stroke of the Foanna would rout the invaders, it had not yet begun to work.

The net holding the extra gear brought along in hopes of Ashe’s escape weighed the Terran down, but he would not abandon it as he felt his way from one foot- and handhold to the next. The waves below gave off an icy exudation which made him shiver uncontrollably. And he knew that as long as that effect lasted he dared not venture into the sea again.

Light﻿ … along with the cold, there was a phosphorescence on the water﻿—white patches floating, dipping, riding the waves. Some of them gathered under the pier, clustering about the pilings. And the fog thinned with their coming, as if those irregular blotches absorbed and fed upon the mist. The Terran could see now he had reached the land end of the jetty. He wedged his flippers into his belt, pulled on over his feet the covers of salkar-hide Torgul had provided.

===Chinese===
福安娜再度吟唱，低沉而清亮。栈桥上的人向海里抛下一些罗斯辨认不出的东西，溅起阵阵水花。特兰人的身体猛地一缩，面罩里闷住了一声痛呼。浸在浪里的双腿和腰腹周围，冷得像火烧一样刺骨。恐惧驱赶着他，让他攀住码头下方的一根横梁向上爬。他爬到那处避难之所，用尽还能召唤出的力气揉搓冻僵的双腿。

过了一会儿，他朝岸上方向匍匐前进。能量射线又找到了一个目标。罗斯停下脚步，眼睁睁看着第二条快艇被切开。如果福安娜的反击能把入侵者击溃，那么此刻它还未显出效力。

那张装着备用装具、为阿什脱身预备的网兜坠得特兰人直往下沉，可他就是不肯扔下它，手脚并用地摸索着从一个支点挪向下一个支点。下方浪涛散发着酷寒的气息，冻得他抖个不停。他知道，只要这种效应还在，他就绝不敢再下海。

光……伴随着寒气，水面上泛起了磷光——一块块白色光斑漂浮着、沉浮着、随浪起伏。有几团聚到码头底下，簇拥在桩柱周围。它们一来，雾就变薄了，仿佛那些形状不规则的光斑正在吸食雾气、以雾为食。特兰人这才看清，自己已到了栈桥的岸端。他把脚蹼别进腰带，套上托尔古尔给的巨鲨皮鞋套。

===Original===
Save for his belt, his trunks, and the gill-pack, Ross’s body was bare and the cold caught at him. But, slinging the carry net over his shoulder, he dropped to the damp sand and stood listening.

The clamor of the attack which had carried all the way offshore to the Rover cruisers had died away. And there were no more claps of thunder. Instead, there was now a thick wash of rain.

No more fire rays as he faced seaward. And the fog was lifting, so Ross could distinguish the settling cutters, their bows still moored to the jetty. There was no movement there. Had those on the pier fled?

Dot﻿ … dash﻿ … dot﻿ …

===Chinese===
除了腰带、短裤和鳃式呼吸包，罗斯身上再无遮蔽，寒气直往骨头里钻。他把网兜往肩上一挎，落身在湿漉漉的沙滩上，站着细听动静。

那一路传到海上掠海者巡航舰里的攻城喧嚣已然消歇。雷声也没有再响。取而代之的，是一场铺天盖地的大雨。

他面朝大海，再没有喷焰光束射来。雾也在散开，罗斯渐渐能看清那几条正在下沉的快艇，船头还系在栈桥边。那里没有任何动静。桥上的人都逃走了吗？

点……划……点……

===Original===
Ross did not drop the net. But he crouched back in the half protection of the piling. For a moment which stretched beyond Terran time measure he froze so, waiting.

Dot﻿ … dash﻿ … dot﻿ …

Not the prickle induced by the enemy installations, it was a real coded call picked up by his sonic, and one he knew.

Don’t rush, he told himself sharply﻿—play it safe. By rights only two people in this time and place would know that call. And one would have no reason to use it. But﻿—a trap? This could be a trap. Awe of the Foanna powers had touched him a little in spite of his off-world skepticism. He could be lured now by someone using Ashe’s call.

===Chinese===
罗斯没有丢下网兜。他缩回桩柱后面，借着它半遮半掩地蹲伏下来。他就这样僵持着等待，那一刻漫长得超出了特兰人对时间的度量。

点……划……点……

这不是敌人装置引起的那种刺麻感，而是他的声波器真切收到的一组电码呼叫——一组他认得的电码。

别急，他严厉地告诫自己——稳妥行事。照理说，在这时代、这地方，只有两个人认得那组呼叫。而其中一个没有理由使用它。可是——陷阱？这可能是陷阱。尽管他对异星巫术素来怀疑，福安娜的神通还是让他心生几分敬畏。此刻他可能正被什么人用阿什的呼号诱出来。

===Original===
Ross stripped for action after a fashion, bundling the net and its contents into a hollow he scooped behind a pile well above water level. The alien hand weapon he had left with Karara, not trusting it to the sea. But he had his diver’s knife and his two hands which, by training, could be, and had been, deadly weapons.

With the sonic against the bare skin of his middle where it would register strongest, knife in hand, Ross moved into the open. The floating patches did not supply much light, but he was certain the call had come from the jetty.

There was movement there﻿—a flash or two. And the sonic? Ross had to be sure, very sure. The broadcast was certainly stronger when he faced in that direction. Dared he come into the open? Perhaps in the dark he could cut Ashe away from his captors so they could swim for it together.

Ross clicked a code reply. Dot﻿ … dot﻿ … dot﻿ …

===Chinese===
罗斯勉强收拾利落，把网兜连同里面的东西塞进一个坑里——那坑挖在一根桩柱后面、远高于水面的地方。那件异星手枪他留给了卡拉拉，不敢托付给海水。但他还有潜水刀，还有一双手——凭受过的训练，这双手既可以是、也曾经是致命的武器。

罗斯把声波器贴在腰腹裸露的皮肤上——那里感应最灵敏——手握尖刀，走进了空旷处。漂浮的光斑提供不了多少亮光，但他确信呼叫来自栈桥。

那边有动静——闪过一两次微光。声波器呢？罗斯必须确定，非常确定。当他朝那个方向转身时，信号显然更强了。他敢暴露在明处吗？也许他能趁黑把阿什从俘他的人手里截出来，两人一起下水逃走。

罗斯敲出一组电码回应。点……点……点……

===Original===
The answer was quick, imperative: “Where?”

Surely no one but Ashe could have sent that! Ross did not hesitate.

“Be ready﻿—escape.”

“No!” Even more imperative. “Friends here.﻿ …”

Had he guessed rightly? Had Ashe established friendly relations with the Foanna? But Ross kept to the caution which had been his defense and armor so long. There was one question he thought only Ashe could answer, something out of the past they had shared when they had made their first journey into time disguised as Beaker traders of the Bronze Age. Deliberately he tapped that question.

===Chinese===
回答来得又快又急迫：「在哪里？」

除了阿什，绝不会有别人发来这句！罗斯不再犹豫。

「准备好——逃。」

「不！」语气更加急迫。「这里有朋友。……」

他猜对了吗？阿什已经和福安娜建立起友好关系了？但罗斯依然守着他长久以来的护身铠甲——谨慎。有一个问题他认为只有阿什答得上来，那出自他们共同经历的往事：他们第一次穿越时间时，曾化装成青铜时代的比克商人。他不慌不忙地敲出了那个问题。

===Original===
“What did we kill in Britain?”

Tensely he waited. But when the reply came it did not pulse from the sonic under his fingers; instead, a well-remembered voice called out of the night.

“A white wolf.” And the words were Terran English.

“Ashe!” Ross leaped forward, climbed toward the figure he could only dimly see.

===Chinese===
「我们在不列颠杀了什么？」

他紧张地等着。可回答传来时，并不是指下声波器的震动，而是夜色中响起一个他记忆犹新的声音。

「一头白狼。」说的是特兰人的英语。

「阿什！」罗斯一跃而上，朝那道他只能隐约看见的身影攀去。

## XIV The Foanna / XIV 福安娜

===Original===
“Ross!” Ashe’s hands gripped his shoulders as if never intending to free him again. “Then you did come through﻿—”

Ross understood. Gordon Ashe must have feared that he was the only one swept through the time door by that freak chance.

“And Karara and the dolphins!”

“Here﻿—now?” In this black bowl of the citadel bay Ashe was only a shadow with voice and hands.

“No, out with the Rover cruisers. Ashe, do you know the Baldies are on Hawaika? They’ve organized this whole thing﻿—the attack here﻿—trouble all over. Right now they have one of their subs out there. That’s what cut those cutters to pieces. Five days ago five of them wiped out a whole Rover fairing, just five of them!”

===Chinese===
「罗斯！」阿什的双手紧紧抓住他的肩膀，仿佛再也不打算放开。「那么你真的也过来了——」

罗斯明白他的心情。戈登·阿什一定一直在担心，那场奇异的变故只把他一个人卷进了时间之门。

「还有卡拉拉和海豚！」

「在这里——现在？」在城塞海湾这一团漆黑的碗里，阿什只是一道有声音、有双手的影子。

「不，他们在外面，跟着掠海者的巡航舰。阿什，你知道秃头人到哈瓦伊卡了吗？这一切都是他们策划的——这里的进攻——还有各地的乱子。此刻他们就有一艘潜艇在外面，把那些快艇切碎的就是它。五天前，他们当中的五个就血洗了整整一场掠海者集会——区区五个人！」

===Original===
“Gordoon.” Unlike the hissing speech of the Hawaikans, this new voice made a singing, lilting call of Ashe’s name. “This is your swordsman in truth?” Another shadow drew near them, and Ross saw the flutter of cloak edge.

“This is my friend.” There was a tone of correction in Ashe’s reply. “Ross, this is the Guardian of the sea gate.”

“And you come,” the Foanna continued, “with those who gather to feast at the Shadow’s table. But your Rovers will find little loot to their liking﻿—”

“No.” Ross hesitated. How did one address the Foanna? He had claimed equality with Torgul. But that approach was not the proper one here; instinct told him that. He fell back on the complete truth uttered simply. “We took three of the Baldy killers. From them we learned they move to wipe out the Foanna first. For you,” he addressed himself to the cloaked shape, “they believe to be a threat. We heard that they urged the Wreckers to this attack and so﻿—”

===Chinese===
「戈尔杜恩。」这新的声音与哈瓦伊卡人那种咝咝作响的说话方式不同，把阿什的名字唤得像一支悠扬婉转的歌。「这位当真就是你的剑士？」又一道影子向他们凑近，罗斯看见斗篷的边缘在飘动。

「这是我的朋友。」阿什的回答里带着一丝纠正的意味。「罗斯，这位是海门的守卫者。」

「而你，」那福安娜继续道，「是与那些到阴影餐桌上赴宴的人同来的。不过你的掠海者朋友们会发现，这里没什么合他们胃口的赃物——」

「不是的。」罗斯迟疑了。该怎么跟福安娜说话？他先前与托尔古尔是以对等身份相称的。但那套做法在这里并不合适——本能这样告诉他。于是他退而采用老老实实、直截了当的全盘真话。「我们抓了三个秃头人杀手。从他们口中得知，秃头人打算先消灭福安娜。因为他们，」他转向那裹着斗篷的身影，「把你们视为威胁。我们听说正是他们煽动海难匪发动这场进攻，所以——」

===Original===
“And so the Rovers come, but not to loot? Then they are something new among their kind.” The Foanna’s reply was as chill as the sea bay’s water.

“Loot does not summon men who want a blood price for their dead kin!” Ross retorted.

“No, and the Rovers are believers in the balance of hurt against hurt,” the Foanna conceded. “Do they also believe in the balance of aid against aid? Now that is a thought upon which depends much. Gordoon, it would seem that we may not take to our ships. So let us return to council.”

===Chinese===
「所以掠海者来了，却不是为了劫掠？那他们可算是本族中的新奇之物了。」福安娜的回答冷得如同这处海湾的海水。

「想为死去的亲人讨还血债的人，可不是劫掠能召唤来的！」罗斯反驳道。

「不错，掠海者信奉以伤害偿还伤害的平衡。」福安娜让步道。「可他们也相信以援助回报援助的平衡吗？这才是一个关系重大的问题。戈尔杜恩，看来我们不能登船出海了。那么，让我们回议事厅去吧。」

===Original===
Ashe’s hand was on Ross’s arm guiding him through the murk. Though the fog which had choked the bay had vanished, thick darkness remained and Ross noted that even the fires and flares were dimmed and fewer. Then they were in a passage where a very faint light clung to the walls.

Robed Foanna, three of them, moved ahead with that particular gliding progress. Then Ashe and Ross, and bringing up the rear, a dozen of the mailed guards. The passageway became a ramp. Ross glanced at Ashe. Like the Foanna, the Terran Agent wore a cloak of gray, but his did not shift color from time to time as did those of the Hawaikan enigmas. And now Gordon shoved back its folds, revealing supple body armor.

Questions gathered in Ross. He wanted to know﻿—needed desperately to know﻿—Ashe’s standing with the Foanna. What had happened to raise Gordon from the status of captive in Zahur’s hold to familiar companionship with the most dreaded race on this planet?

The ramp’s head faced blank wall with a sharp-angled turn to the right of a narrower passage. One of the Foanna made a slight sign to the guards, who turned with drilled precision to march off along the passage. Now the other Foanna held out their wands.

===Chinese===
阿什的手搭在罗斯臂上，引着他穿过昏暗。虽然先前堵住海湾的浓雾已经散去，沉沉的黑夜依旧，罗斯还注意到，连火光和信号焰都变得黯淡稀少了。随后他们进入一条通道，四壁附着一层极淡的微光。

三个罩着长袍的福安娜走在前面，行进间带着那种特有的飘然滑步。接着是阿什和罗斯，殿后的则是一打披甲的卫兵。通道渐渐变成一道坡道。罗斯瞥了阿什一眼。和福安娜一样，这位特兰特工也披着灰色斗篷，只是他的斗篷不像那些哈瓦伊卡之谜的衣袍那样时时变换颜色。此刻戈登把褶层向两边一撩，露出里面柔软贴身的铠甲。

疑问在罗斯心里越积越多。他想知道——迫切地想知道——阿什在福安娜这里是什么身份。究竟发生了什么，让戈登从扎胡尔牢里的俘虏，一跃成为这颗行星上最令人畏惧的种族的亲近同伴？

坡道顶端直面一堵光秃秃的墙，右手边急转过去是一条更窄的通道。一名福安娜朝卫兵做了个轻微的手势，卫兵们便训练有素地齐刷刷转身，沿通道列队离去。这时另外两名福安娜举起了她们的长杖。

===Original===
What a moment earlier had been unbroken surface showed an opening. The change had been so instantaneous that Ross had not seen any movement at all.

Beyond that door they passed from one world to another. Ross’s senses, already acutely alert to his surroundings, could not supply him with any reason by sight, sound, or smell for his firm conviction that this hold was alien as neither the Wrecker castle nor the Rover ships had been. Surely the Foanna were not the same race, perhaps not even the same species as the other native Hawaikans.

Those robes which he had seen both silver gray and dark blue, now faded, pearled, thinned, until each of the three still gliding before him were opalescent columns without definite form.

===Chinese===
方才还浑然一体的墙面上豁然现出一道开口。变化来得如此之快，罗斯根本没看见任何动作。

跨过那道门，他们便从一个世界进入了另一个世界。罗斯的感官本已对周遭高度警觉，却无法从视觉、听觉或嗅觉中找到任何理由，来解释他心中那个笃定的念头：这座要塞是异质的，海难匪的城堡也好、掠海者的船也好，都不曾给他这种感觉。福安娜肯定不是同一个种族，甚至可能与哈瓦伊卡其他土著连物种都不相同。

那些长袍，他先前看到的时而是银灰、时而是深蓝，此刻淡去、泛出珠光、变得稀薄，直到前方飘行的三人各自化作一根光华流转的柱影，再无确定的形体。

===Original===
Ashe’s grasp fell on Ross’s arm once more, and his whisper reached the younger man thinly. “They are mistresses of illusion. Be prepared not to believe all that you see.”

Mistresses﻿—Ross caught that first. Women, or at least female then. Illusion, yes, already he was convinced that here his eyes could play tricks on him. He could hardly determine what was robe, what was wall, or if more than shades of shades swept before him.

Another blank wall, then an opening, and flowing through it to touch him such a wave of alienness that Ross felt he was buffeted by a storm wind. Yet as he hesitated before it, reluctant in spite of Ashe’s hold to go ahead, he also knew that this did not carry with it the cold hostility he had known while facing the Baldies. Alien﻿—yes. Inimical to his kind﻿—no.

===Chinese===
阿什的手再次落在罗斯臂上，他压低的耳语细细传来。「她们是幻术的女主人。要有准备，别尽信你看到的一切。」

女主人——罗斯首先抓住了这个词。是女人，或者至少是雌性。幻术，不错，他已经确信在这里自己的眼睛会跟他耍花招。他几乎分不清什么是长袍、什么是墙壁，也分不清眼前掠过的究竟是不是影子的影子。

又是一堵光秃秃的墙，然后是一道开口，一股异质之感如潮水般从中涌出，扑在罗斯身上，打得他像迎面挨了暴风。可当他迟疑着不肯上前——尽管有阿什拉着，仍旧踌躇——的时候，他也明白这股感觉里并没有他面对秃头人时体会到的那种冰冷敌意。异质——是的。敌视他的同类——不。

===Original===
“You are right, younger brother.”

Spoken those words﻿—or forming in his mind?

Ross was in a place which was sheer wonder. Under his feet dark blue﻿—the blue of a Terran sky at dusk﻿—caught up in it twinkling points of light as if he strode, not equal with stars, but above them! Walls﻿—were there any walls here? Or shifting, swaying blue curtains on which silvery lines ran to form symbols and words which some bemused part of his brain almost understood, but not quite.

===Chinese===
「你想得对，弟弟。」

这些话是说出来的——还是在他心里成形的？

罗斯置身于一个纯然不可思议的地方。脚下是深蓝色——特拉天空黄昏时分的蓝——蓝色里缀着点点闪烁的光，仿佛他不是与繁星并肩，而是高踞群星之上迈步而行！墙壁——这里有墙壁吗？还是一幕幕摇曳变幻的蓝色帷幔，银线在幔上流走，织出符号与文字，他头脑中某个痴迷的部分几乎就要读懂，却总差那么一点。

===Original===
Constant motion, no quiet, until he came to a place where those swaying curtains were stilled, where he no longer strode above the sky but on soft surface, a mat of gray living sod where his steps released a spicy fragrance. And there he really saw the Foanna for the first time.

Where had their cloaks gone? Had they tossed them away during that walk or drift across this amazing room, or had the substance which had formed those coverings flowed away by itself? As Ross looked at the three in wonder he knew that he was seeing them as not even their servants and guards ever viewed them. And yet was he seeing them as they really were or as they wished him to see them?

“As we are, younger brother, as we are!” Again an answer which Ross was not sure was thought or speech.

In form they were humanoid, and they were undoubtedly women. The muffling cloaks gone, they wore sleeveless garments of silver which were girdled at the waist with belts of blue gems. Only in their hair and their eyes did they betray alien blood. For the hair which flowed and wove about them, cascading down shoulders, rippling about their arms, was silver, too, and it swirled, moved as if it had a separate life of its own. While their eyes.﻿ … Ross looked into those golden eyes and was lost for seconds until panic awoke in him, forcing him after sharp struggle to look away.

===Chinese===
一直在动，没有一刻安宁，直到他来到一处帷幔静止的地方，在那里他不再是凌空于天穹之上，而是踩在柔软的地面上——一块灰色的活草皮织成的垫子，脚步落下便释放出辛辣的香气。在那里，他才第一次真正看清了福安娜。

她们的长袍到哪里去了？是穿越这个奇异厅堂的一路上被她们随手抛掉了，还是织成那些衣袍的物质自己流散了？罗斯惊叹地打量着这三人，心知自己此刻见到的是连她们的仆从和卫兵都从未见过的模样。然而，他看到的是她们的真实模样，还是她们愿他看到的样子？

「就是我们的本来样子，弟弟，本来样子！」又是一个回答，罗斯依旧分不清那是意念还是言语。

她们形似人类，而且毫无疑问是女人。遮身蔽体的长袍褪去后，她们穿着银色的无袖长衣，腰间束着蓝宝石的腰带。唯有头发和眼睛泄露了她们的异族血统。那披拂缠绕的长发如瀑布泻过肩头、如涟漪漾过臂弯，也是一片银色，盘旋涌动，仿佛自有独立的生命。还有她们的眼睛。……罗斯望进那双金色的眼眸，一失神便是好几秒，直到恐慌在心中惊醒，经过一番痛苦的挣扎才强迫自己移开目光。

===Original===
Laughter? No, he had not heard laughter. But a sense of amusement tinged with respect came to him.

“You are very right, Gordoon. This one is also of your kind. He is not witches’ meat.” Ross caught the distaste, the kind of haunting unhappiness which colored those words, remnants of an old hurt.

“These are the Foanna,” Ashe’s voice broke more of the spell. “The Lady Ynlan, The Lady Yngram, the Lady Ynvalda.”

===Chinese===
笑声？不，他没有听到笑声。但一种带着敬意的愉悦之感传到了他心里。

「你说得很对，戈尔杜恩。这一个也是你的同类。他不是女巫之肉。」罗斯听出了那话音里的厌恶，还有一种萦绕不去的哀伤，为这些字句染上了色——那是旧日创伤的余痕。

「这几位就是福安娜。」阿什的声音打散了更多魔咒。「伊恩兰女士、伊恩格拉姆女士、伊恩瓦尔达女士。」

===Original===
The Foanna﻿—these three only?

She whom Ashe had named Ynlan, whose eyes had entrapped and almost held what was Ross Murdock, made a small gesture with her ivory hand. And in that gesture as well as in the words witches’ meat the Terran read the unhappiness which was as much a part of this room as the rest of its mystery.

“The Foanna are now but three. They have been only three for many weary years, oh man from another world and time. And soon, if these enemies have their way, they will not be three﻿—but none!”

“But﻿—” Ross was still startled. He knew from Loketh that the Wreckers had deemed the Foanna few in number, an old and dying race. But that there were only three women left was hard to believe.

===Chinese===
福安娜——就只有这三位？

被阿什称作伊恩兰的那一位——正是她的眼眸困住了罗斯·默多克的自我、险些将它据为己有——用象牙色的手做了个小手势。从那手势里，也和从「女巫之肉」这几个字里一样，特兰人读出了那种哀伤；它和这个房间的其余种种神秘一样，是这里浑然一体的一部分。

「福安娜如今只剩三人。多少个凄凉的年头里，我们一直都只有三人，来自另一个世界、另一个时代的凡人啊。而不久之后，如果任由这些敌人得逞，我们连三人也不是——而是零！」

「可是——」罗斯仍觉愕然。他从洛凯斯那里知道，海难匪认为福安娜人数寥寥，是个衰老垂死的种族。可只剩三个女人，这实在难以置信。

===Original===
The response to his unspoken wonder came clear and determined. “We may be but three; however, our power remains. And sometimes power distilled by time becomes the stronger. Now it would seem that time is no longer our servant but perhaps among our enemies. So tell us this tale of yours as to why the Rovers would make one with the Foanna﻿—tell us all, younger brother!”

Ross reported what he had seen, what Tino-rau and Taua had learned from the prisoners taken at Kyn Add. And when he had finished, the three Foanna stood very still, their hands clasped one to the other. Though they were only an arm’s distance from him, Ross had the feeling they had withdrawn from his time and world.

So complete was their withdrawal that he dared to ask Ashe one of the many questions which had been boiling inside him.

===Chinese===
对他未说出口的惊疑，回答来得干脆而决断。「我们或许只有三人，然而我们的力量犹在。而经时间蒸馏淬炼的力量，有时反而愈发强大。只是如今，时间似乎已不再为我们效力，反倒站到我们的敌人那边去了。那么，把你们的故事讲给我们听吧——说说掠海者为何愿与福安娜合为一家——原原本本都讲出来，弟弟！」

罗斯讲述了自己的所见所闻，讲了蒂诺-劳和塔乌亚从基恩-阿德的俘虏那里探知的一切。他讲完之后，三位福安娜静静伫立，彼此手手相扣。虽然她们离他不过一臂之遥，罗斯却觉得她们已从他的时代和他的世界里退了出去。

她们退得如此彻底，他竟敢趁机向阿什问起那个在心里翻腾已久的诸多问题之一。

===Original===
“Who are they?” But Ross knew he really meant: What are they?

Gordon Ashe shook his head. “I don’t really know﻿—the last of a very old race which possesses powers and knowledge different from any we have believed in for centuries. We have heard of witches. In the modern day we discount the legends about them. The Foanna bring those legends alive. And I promise you this﻿—if they turn those powers loose”﻿—he paused﻿—“it will be such a war as this world, perhaps any world has never seen!”

“That is so.” The Foanna had returned from the place to which they had withdrawn. “And this is also the truth or one face of the truth. The Rovers are right in their belief that we have kept some measure of balance between one form of change and another on this world. If we were as many as we once were, then against us these invaders could not move at all. But we are three only and also﻿—do we have the right to evoke disaster which will strike not only the enemy but perhaps recoil upon the innocent? There has been enough death here already. And those who are our servants shall no longer be asked to face battle to keep an empty shell inviolate. We would see with our own eyes these invaders, probe what they would do. There is ever change in life, and if a pattern grows too set, then the race caught in it may wither and die. Maybe our pattern has been too long in its old design. We shall make no decision until we see in whose hands the future may rest.”

Against such finality of argument there was no appeal. These could not be influenced by words.

===Chinese===
「她们是谁？」但罗斯知道自己真正想问的是：她们是什么？

戈登·阿什摇了摇头。「我也不真明白——这是一个非常古老的种族最后的血脉，她们掌握的力量与知识，与我们几个世纪以来所相信的任何东西都不同。我们听说过女巫。在现代，我们把关于她们的传说当作无稽之谈。而福安娜让那些传说活了过来。我可以向你保证——一旦她们把那些力量放出来，」他顿了顿，「那将是一场这样的战争，这个世界、也许任何世界都从未见过的战争！」

「确实如此。」福安娜已从她们退去的地方回来了。「这也是真相，或者说是真相的一面。掠海者相信，我们在这个世界上维持着各种变迁之间某种程度的平衡，他们信得没错。倘若我们还有昔日的数量，这些入侵者根本动不了我们分毫。可我们只有三人，而且——我们有权唤来一场灾难吗？它不但会打击敌人，说不定还会反噬无辜。这片土地上死的人已经够多了。我们的仆从也不该再被要求去作战，只为保全一具空壳完好无损。我们要亲眼看看这些入侵者，探一探他们的图谋。生命本就处于无尽的变迁之中，而一种格局若凝固得太久，困在其中的种族便可能枯萎、死亡。或许我们的格局，守着旧设计守得太久了。在看清未来可能落入谁的手中之前，我们不做任何决定。」

面对如此决绝的论断，无从再辩。这样的存在，不是言语所能打动的。

===Original===
“Gordoon, there is much to be done. Do you take with you this younger brother and see to his needs. When all is in readiness we shall come.”

One minute Ross had been standing on the carpet of living moss. Then﻿ … he was in a more normal room with four walls, a floor, a ceiling, and light which came from rods set in the corners. He gasped.

“Stunned me, too, the first time they put me through it,” he heard Ashe say. “Here, get some of this inside you, it’ll steady your head.”

===Chinese===
「戈尔杜恩，有许多事要做。你带上这个弟弟，去照应他的需要。一切准备停当后，我们自会前来。」

前一分钟罗斯还站在活苔藓织成的地毯上。然后……他已置身于一间寻常得多的房间，有四壁、地板、天花板，光亮来自嵌在四角的灯棒。他倒抽一口气。

「头一回她们带我这么过来时，我也吓懵了。」他听见阿什说。「来，把这个喝下去一点，能让你的脑子定一定。」

===Original===
There was a cup in his hand, a beautifully carved, rose-red container shaped in the form of a flower. Somehow Ross brought it to his lips with shaking hands, gulped down a good third of its contents. The liquid was a mixture of tart and sweet, cooling his mouth and throat, but warming as it went down, and that glow spread through him.

“What﻿—how did they do that?” he demanded.

Ashe shrugged. “How do they do the hundred and one things I have seen happen here? We’ve been teleported. How it’s done I don’t know any more than I did the first time it happened. Simply a part of Foanna ‘magic’ as far as spectators are concerned.” He sat down on a stool, his long legs stretched out before him. “Other worlds, other ways﻿—even if they are confounded queer ones.
